Conclusion: 个性化正在成为 LLM 智能体的定义性能力，推动其从通用任务执行迈向长期、用户对齐的协作。本文通过四大能力（画像建模、记忆、规划、行动执行）提供了系统级理解框架，指出个性化不是孤立模块而是智能体设计的系统级属性。总体而言，仍有待解决的核心挑战包括：决策关键的用户建模（区分决策相关属性与外围属性）、时间动态性与持续个性化（避免灾难性遗忘、区分短期偏好与长期价值）、稀疏数据下的泛化（少样本/元学习、不确定性感知、可迁移偏好抽象）、评测与基准（合成用户同质化、LLM-as-a-judge 可靠性、需区分个体内一致性与个体间差异性、引入人在环与纵向研究）、隐私与用户控制（隐私感知记忆、选择性留存、端侧/联邦个性化、可编辑透明接口）、以及效率与部署成本权衡（轻量化个性化、自适应记忆、预算感知推理）。未来进展需要集成化架构，在个性化之外同时保证鲁棒性、安全性与可部署性，从而推动该领域从原型级个性化走向可扩展的现实世界助手。

Abstract: Large language models have enabled agents that reason, plan, and interact with tools and environments to accomplish complex tasks. As these agents operate over extended interaction horizons, their effectiveness increasingly depends on adapting behavior to individual users and maintaining continuity across time, giving rise to personalized LLM-powered agents. In such long-term, user-dependent settings, personalization permeates the entire decision pipeline rather than remaining confined to surface-level generation. This survey provides a capability-oriented review of personalized LLM-powered agents. We organize the literature around four interdependent components: profile modeling, memory, planning, and action execution. Using this taxonomy, we synthesize representative methods and analyze how user signals are represented, propagated, and utilized, highlighting cross-component interactions and recurring design trade-offs. We further examine evaluation metrics and benchmarks tailored to personalized agents, summarize application scenarios spanning general assistance to specialized domains, and outline future directions for research and deployment. By offering a structured framework for understanding and designing personalized LLM-powered agents, this survey charts a roadmap toward more user-aligned, adaptive, robust, and deployable agentic systems, accelerating progress from prototype personalization to scalable real-world assistants.

</details>


### [70] [Knob: A Physics-Inspired Gating Interface for Interpretable and Controllable Neural Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22702)
*Siyu Jiang,Sanshuai Cui,Hui Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Knob 把神经网络的门控机制建模为一个经典的二阶阻尼机械系统（质量-弹簧-阻尼器），用阻尼比 ζ（稳定性/保守性）与自然频率 ω_n（灵敏度/带宽）作为面向操作员的"旋钮"。核心是 logit 层的凸融合（等价于输入自适应温度缩放），并用 Tustin 离散化保证步长无关稳定性，支持 Reset（i.i.d.）与 Continuous（流式）双模式推理。在 CIFAR-10-C 上验证：ODE-Lite 校准–成本最优，Knob-IA 精度最高，Knob-ODE 在连续模式下呈现阶跃整定与低通衰减等标准二阶控制特性。论文定位为探索性架构接口，不主张 SOTA 校准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有校准方法（后验温度缩放、标签平滑、Mixup 等）本质是静态、事后优化，且依赖与测试分布相似的验证集，在非平稳数据流下退化；更关键的是它们"不透明"——部署后出现异常行为时操作员没有任何直观可调的杠杆，往往只能重新训练或重校准。作者主张把推理看作一个可控的动力系统（类比调音台旋钮、悬挂调校），用经典力学/控制论的阻尼、刚度、惯量作为可解释的调节语言，并进一步主张第三条路线——"面向校准的架构设计"，用显式的控制论先验替代 ViT 等架构中偶然涌现的校准特性。

Method: 1) 双流骨干：共享编码器 + 两个轻量投影头，输出 z_static 与 z_dyn；2) 凸融合：z_fuse = g(x)·z_dyn + (1-g(x))·z_static，g∈[0,1]，样本级标量门；3) 物理引擎：对隐变量 u 施加二阶阻尼 ODE（ü + 2ζω_n u̇ + ω_n² u = ω_n² u*(x)），g = σ(u)，写成状态空间 x=[u, u̇]ᵀ，经 Tustin（双线性）变换离散化为 A_d、B_d，Proposition 1 证明 ζ>0、ω_n>0 时对任意 Δt>0 特征值均 |λ|<1（步长无关稳定性）；4) 双模式推理：Reset Mode 每个样本重置 x0=0（单步自适应门，ODE 仍带来参数化收缩的正则化效果），Continuous Mode 跨时间步保留状态实现物理惯性与低通滤波；5) 方法家族：Static、Attention（非凸对照）、Knob-IA（可学习凸门）、ODE-Lite（一阶 EMA 门）、Knob-ODE（完整二阶动力学）。理论方面：Proposition 2/3 证明凸融合使 Top-2 间隔 m_fuse = g·m1+(1-g)·m2 ∈ [min, max]，从而收缩到 ≤ max(m1,m2)，对应有效温度 T_eff = max(m1,m2)/m_fuse ≥ 1，且分支间差距越大温度越高；定义置信收缩比 CSR = s(max(m1,m2)/T_eff)/s(max(m1,m2))；Appendix C 证明固定分支 logit 时交叉熵关于 g 为凸函数（整体训练仍非凸）。开销仅 O(D)（u*(x) 为轻量 MLP，ζ、ω_n 为标量）。

Result: CIFAR-10-C（19 种腐蚀 × 5 强度，ResNet-18，3 次平均，Reset Mode）：Static Avg-C 71.25 / Err-C 28.75 / ECE_deb 0.1553 / 3.48ms；Attention 70.45 / 29.55 / 0.1648 / 3.51ms；Knob-IA 72.52 / 27.48 / 0.1617 / 3.49ms；ODE-Lite 71.62 / 28.38 / 0.1488（最佳）/ 3.64ms；Knob-ODE 71.49 / 28.51 / 0.1829 / 4.41ms。参数与 GFLOPs 基本一致（11.70–11.90M，0.5584–0.5586）。E-1：CSR < 1 且随腐蚀强度下降，T̂(x) ≥ 1 且随强度上升（Spearman ρ=0.6），分歧子集最明显，可靠性图显示分歧样本更保守；E-2：门控与优势指标对齐的 ROC-AUC 随训练上升（epoch 50 时 AUC_OA≈0.79、AUC_GCA≈0.74），极化指数 Π≈0.10，说明能"择优"但不饱和；E-3（Continuous Mode，固定图像+时变腐蚀强度的受控流）：阶跃响应下 g(t) 呈平滑阻尼过渡而非瞬时跳变，输出置信 p_max 随之调节；正弦扫频的 Bode 幅度相对 DC 呈下降趋势（负 dB），符合"软"低通滤波，但衰减曲线比理想连续系统更平缓。作者明确声明不主张 SOTA 校准性能，只验证控制论性质。

Conclusion: Knob 证明了一条可行的设计轴：用物理/控制论显式约束神经门控动态，可以在静态场景通过凸融合温和化置信度、在动态场景通过二阶阻尼实现平滑可预期的过渡，并使运行期调参（"稳定性"与"灵敏度"）在结构上可行。局限：仅在 CIFAR-10/CIFAR-10-C 验证；依赖双流骨干，难以直接套用既有单流模型；未做运行期调参的用户研究或系统参数扫描；Knob-ODE 的二阶优势仅在时间相关的 Continuous Mode 下体现，i.i.d. 场景更推荐 ODE-Lite/Knob-IA。后续方向包括：单流模型上的时序自集成、扩展到高阶系统（如 PID）、面向真实人机团队的用户研究、扩展到 ImageNet-C 等更大规模基准。

Abstract: Existing neural network calibration methods often treat calibration as a static, post-hoc optimization task. However, this neglects the dynamic and temporal nature of real-world inference. Moreover, existing methods do not provide an intuitive interface enabling human operators to dynamically adjust model behavior under shifting conditions. In this work, we propose Knob, a framework that connects deep learning with classical control theory by mapping neural gating dynamics to a second-order mechanical system. By establishing correspondences between physical parameters -- damping ratio ($ζ$) and natural frequency ($ω_n$) -- and neural gating, we create a tunable "safety valve". The core mechanism employs a logit-level convex fusion, functioning as an input-adaptive temperature scaling. It tends to reduce model confidence particularly when model branches produce conflicting predictions. Furthermore, by imposing second-order dynamics (Knob-ODE), we enable a \textit{dual-mode} inference: standard i.i.d. processing for static tasks, and state-preserving processing for continuous streams. Our framework allows operators to tune "stability" and "sensitivity" through familiar physical analogues. This paper presents an exploratory architectural interface; we focus on demonstrating the concept and validating its control-theoretic properties rather than claiming state-of-the-art calibration performance. Experiments on CIFAR-10-C validate the calibration mechanism and demonstrate that, in Continuous Mode, the gate responses are consistent with standard second-order control signatures (step settling and low-pass attenuation), paving the way for predictable human-in-the-loop tuning.

</details>


### [71] [Generative Data Transformation: From Mixed to Unified Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22743)
*Jiaqing Zhang,Mingjia Yin,Hao Wang,Yuxin Tian,Yuyang Ye,Yawen Li,Wei Guo,Yong Liu,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出名为 Taesar（Target-aligned Sequential Regeneration）的跨域序列推荐数据中心框架：不设计复杂的跨域融合网络，而是在训练前通过"三模型对比解码"把混合域的用户行为序列重写为只含目标域物品的纯净序列，从而在数据层面对齐语义、规避负迁移；生成的数据可直接喂给任意单域序列推荐器，无需改动模型结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多域数据混合虽能缓解单域稀疏与冷启动，但域间分布异质性/语义鸿沟会导致梯度冲突与负迁移（论文用四域梯度方向余弦相似度热力图佐证）。主流"模型中心"范式（Pi-Net、PSJNet、C2DSR、DREAM、UniSRec、ABXI、SyNCRec 等）依赖定制化融合模块与多任务训练，存在三点不足：(1) 统一表示空间的多域联合建模带来高优化复杂度与训练成本，反而损害单域精度；(2) 融合模块强绑定模型，通用性与可扩展性差；(3) 无法捕捉跨域间细微、非结构性的序列依赖，资源开销大。作者据此提出核心问题：如何既利用跨域分散行为以增强目标域推荐，又避免负迁移？并主张把多域建模问题在数据层面降维为精确的单域问题。

Method: **问题形式化**：域 D=(U,V,E)，将用户跨域交互按时间 Interleave 成混合序列 x^M；定义重生成算子 Regenerate(·)，把源域物品经 f_m: S_m→T∪{∅} 映射或丢弃，得到只含目标域物品的 x^N，最终训练集 N = x^N ∪ x^T（保留原目标域序列 + 重生成序列）。

**第一阶段：预训练与域特定适配**
- 跨域编码器预训练：在混合序列上以交叉熵训练 Transformer 类解码器 θ_M，捕获跨域共享行为规律并把各域物品映射到统一语义空间。
- 域特定解码器适配：采用 DSP（Domain-Specific Prediction）策略——输入为完整混合域历史，但仅预测当前域的物品；对目标域专家，其有效预测对 P_T(x) 要求 x_i、x_t 均属目标域、t>i 且 (i,t) 之间不存在其他目标域物品（即"下一个出现的"目标域物品）；源域专家同理。这既继承预训练的跨域知识，又塑造域专长。

**第二阶段：自适应对比解码（Expert-Amateur 范式的三模型扩展）**
- 角色：目标域专家为"强专家/监督者"，源域专家为"业余者"（用于过滤有害跨域偏置），基座模型用于度量可迁移知识。
- 全局对比分数（在整个物品空间 V 上）：Global = α_g·P^T − β_g·P^{S_k}，其最大值落在目标域物品上说明该源域物品可迁移且目标专家有把握。
- 局部对比分数（限定在目标域词表 V^T 上）：Local = α_l·P^T_{V^T} − β_l·P^{S_k}_{V^T}，用于精确定位替换目标。
- 自适应权重：α 由目标专家输出分布的 Shannon 熵归一化得到（低熵=高置信，权重更大）；β 由基座模型与源域专家分布间的 Jensen–Shannon 散度（以 KL 的对称组合实现）得到（JSD 低=跨域对齐强=可迁移性高）。
- 决策：对混合序列中每个源域物品，基于全局/局部两类对比分数决定"替换为目标域物品"还是"直接丢弃"，最终生成保持时间顺序、仅含目标域物品的纯净序列。

**设计动机与定位**：区别于数据蒸馏（压缩为紧凑合成表示）、数据生成（从学习到的分布合成新样本）与常规数据增强（随机/语义变换），Taesar 属于数据重生成（DR），遵循 X→X′→Y 的隐式过渡思路，参考 DR4SR；也是首次将对比解码用于序列推荐的数据重生成。

Result: **实验设置**：Amazon 公开评论数据四个域（Books、Electronics、Sports、Tools），预处理沿用 Park et al. 2024 与 Bian et al. 2025（用户/物品最少 3 次交互、保留最近 128 条），数据极度稀疏（99.97%–99.98%）。基线：单域目标模型 SASRec、GRU4Rec、GCE-GNN、CL4SRec；跨域基线 SASRec_4（朴素合并）、ABXI、CGRec、SyNCRec。评测采用 leave-one-out，且按 Krichene & Rendle 的意见改用全量物品集计算 HR@k / NG@k / MRR@k（k=10,20），避免采样指标失真。

**主要结论**：
1. 朴素混合数据确实产生负迁移：SASRec_4 在 Books/Electronics/Sports/Tools 上均低于单域 SASRec（如 Books HR@10 0.0504 vs 0.0563）。
2. 模型中心方法提升有限且不稳定（ABXI、SyNCRec、CGRec 仅在部分指标上偶有胜出，总体偏弱），说明弱跨域语义下架构增强难以弥合差异。
3. Taesar 生成的纯净化序列给所有架构带来一致显著提升：Books 上 GCE-GNN+Taesar HR@10 0.0774（vs 单域 0.0598）、CL4SRec+Taesar 0.0772（vs 0.0585）；Electronics 上 SASRec+Taesar 0.0441（vs 单域 0.0350、朴素合并 0.0311）；Sports、Tools 上同样领先，如 Sports CL4SRec+Taesar HR@10 0.0602 vs 单域 0.0413。
4. 跨架构通用性：再生数据对 RNN/GNN/注意力/对比学习四类模型均有效，且表达能力越强的模型增益越大，说明高质量数据放大了架构优势。
5. 消融（Electronics）：去掉 DSA、DSP、SDE、只用 GCS 或只用 LCS 均显著下降（HR@10 0.0352/0.0350/0.0398/0.0439/0.0431 vs 完整 0.0441），验证三模型对比与双层对比分数缺一不可。
6. 分析：被选中转换的源域物品在嵌入空间中与对应目标物品高度对齐，说明确实检测到了域共享语义；再生后 Books 域物品频率曲线更平坦、长尾覆盖提升（Top10% 占比下降、Mid40% 与 Tail50% 上升）、用户序列更长更密集，从而提供更丰富的时序上下文。

Conclusion: Taesar 以"数据变换优先于架构设计"的思路，通过目标对齐的序列重生成在数据层面化解跨域语义鸿沟：借助自适应对比解码识别可迁移的源域物品并将其转换为语义对齐的目标域物品，在保持时间动态的同时剔除潜在有害的跨域信息。实验显示其在多个域与多种架构上达到 SOTA，且无需修改下游模型即可无缝接入既有序列推荐器，说明数据中心与模型中心两种范式具有互补性——后者提升结构表达力，前者从根本上强化学习信号，从而给出更稳健、可解释的跨域知识迁移方案。局限与展望：作者承认在"重生成过程中信息保持的极限"上缺乏更深层的理论刻画，未来工作将完善数据中心推荐的理论基础与实际适用性。

Abstract: Recommendation model performance is intrinsically tied to the quality, volume, and relevance of their training data. To address common challenges like data sparsity and cold start, recent researchs have leveraged data from multiple auxiliary domains to enrich information within the target domain. However, inherent domain gaps can degrade the quality of mixed-domain data, leading to negative transfer and diminished model performance. Existing prevailing \emph{model-centric} paradigm -- which relies on complex, customized architectures -- struggles to capture the subtle, non-structural sequence dependencies across domains, leading to poor generalization and high demands on computational resources. To address these shortcomings, we propose \textsc{Taesar}, a \emph{data-centric} framework for \textbf{t}arget-\textbf{a}lign\textbf{e}d \textbf{s}equenti\textbf{a}l \textbf{r}egeneration, which employs a contrastive decoding mechanism to adaptively encode cross-domain context into target-domain sequences. It employs contrastive decoding to encode cross-domain context into target sequences, enabling standard models to learn intricate dependencies without complex fusion architectures. Experiments show \textsc{Taesar} outperforms model-centric solutions and generalizes to various sequential models. By generating enriched datasets, \textsc{Taesar} effectively combines the strengths of data- and model-centric paradigms. The code accompanying this paper is available at~ \textcolor{blue}{https://github.com/USTC-StarTeam/Taesar}.

</details>


### [72] [Know What You Know: Metacognitive Entropy Calibration for Verifiable RL Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22751)
*Qiannian Zhao,Chen Yang,Jinhao Jing,Yunke Zhang,Xuhui Ren,Lu Yu,Shijie Zhang,Hongzhi Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 EGPO（Entropy-Guided Policy Optimization），在不改验证器与二元结果奖励的前提下，用基于 token 似然的零开销 NLL 熵代理度量模型内在不确定性，并通过「组内相对熵权重 + 结果感知的非对称校准 + 全错组的熵阻尼负样本强化（NSR）」重标定优势幅度，从而在 GRPO 式 RLVR 中实现"知道自己知道什么"。在 Qwen2.5-Math 与 DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen（1.5B/7B）上，EGPO 在 MATH-500、AIME24、MMLU-STEM、Minerva、GSM8K 上几乎全面优于 GRPO/DAPO/EDGE-GRPO，且正确与错误 rollout 的熵分离度最大（Δ=0.048）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大推理模型（LRM）主要依赖可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR）训练，但现有 outcome-only RLVR（如 GRPO/DAPO/Dr.GRPO/GSPO）几乎只使用二元正确性信号，忽视了模型自身的内在不确定性，论文称之为「不确定性—奖励错配（uncertainty–reward mismatch）」。作者按「正确性 × 不确定性」把学习状态划分为四象限：低不确定正确（真正会做）、高不确定正确（答案对但推理脆弱）、低不确定错误（幻觉/稳定误解）、高不确定错误（探索性尝试）。二元奖励使这四类被同等对待，导致两大缺陷：(1) 无法区分「巩固知识」与「保留探索」的边界，失败样本上的激进更新会压制探索；(2) 在全错组（advantage collapse，GRPO 优势恒为 0）上浪费最难的训练信号，只能过滤或注入外部监督（偏离 outcome-only 设定）。已有最接近的工作 EDGE-GRPO 仅用熵做整体重加权，正误轨迹的缩放幅度可比，无法同时实现「巩固正确」与「保留探索」。作者借元认知（metacognition）启发：不确定性应作为策略更新的调制信号——成功态反映证据强度、决定知识巩固程度，错误态作为阻尼因子抑制过激惩罚、保护探索。

Method: EGPO 保持验证器与奖励定义不变，仅改造组内优势构造。核心组件：(1) 熵代理：H̃(x,y)=-(1/T)Σ log π_old(y_t|x,y_<t)，即旧策略下逐 token 平均 NLL；token 对数概率在 rollout 时已计算，故几乎零额外开销，且无需跨 prompt 的全局校准。(2) 组内相对熵权重：ŵ_i = H̄/(H̃_i+ε_H)，均值归一的逆熵比，组级尺度保持、在组内按相对不确定性重新分配更新幅度（稳健、verifier-agnostic）。(3) 结果感知的非对称校准：正确轨迹 w_i = max(1.0, clip(ŵ_i, λ_min, λ_max))，绝不降权（放大高置信正确样本）；错误轨迹 w_i = min(1.0, clip(ŵ_i, λ_min, λ_max))，绝不升权（避免不确定失败产生过大负梯度），实现 λ_min=0.8、λ_max=2.0。(4) 基础优势分三种组型：全对→0（跳过），混合组→标准 GRPO 组内标准化优势，全错→常数 -1 的 NSR（Negative Sample Reinforcement），恢复非零学习信号。(5) 校准优势 Ã_i = w_i·A_i，代入加权裁剪比率目标 L_EGPO = E[min(ρ_i Ã_i, clip(ρ_i,1±ε)Ã_i)]，在单一 PPO 式目标内统一处理混合组与全错组。附录给出全错组的裁剪与梯度推导：负优势下裁剪仅在 ρ<1-ε 区域生效，其余退化为 -w_i ρ_i，梯度 ∝ w_i ρ_i(1[v=y_t]-π_v)，即按熵调节的负向信号，把被采样 token 概率压低而不摧毁整体探索。可选组内重归一化（w_i ← w_i/mean(w)），消融显示「先裁剪后归一化」优于反向顺序，但整体平均未超过默认设置，故默认不启用。实验设置：OpenR1-Math 派生、过滤去噪并 MinHash-LSH 去重的 OpenR1-Stable-10k 训练集，verl 框架，N=16 rollouts、5 epoch（约 770 步）、lr=1e-6，evalscope 评测，temperature=0.6/top-p=0.95/top-k=20，每基准 8 次独立试验取平均 pass@1。

Result: RQ1（主结果）：EGPO 在四个骨干、五个基准上几乎全面领先。Qwen2.5-Math-7B：MATH-500 84.60（Base 13.80，+70.80；超最强 RL 基线 EDGE-GRPO +3.10）、AIME24 33.33（+30.00，+2.91）、MMLU-STEM 70.09（+46.04，+3.01）、Minerva 44.49（+15.08）、GSM8K 89.84（+9.40）。DeepSeek-R1-7B：93.40 / 66.67 / 91.98 / 83.46 / 93.18，相对最强基线分别 +2.00 / +6.67 / +4.77 / +2.58 / +2.13；基线在 AIME24/MMLU-STEM 上出现零或负增益，EGPO 仍显著提升。DeepSeek-R1-1.5B 全部第一；Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B 五项中四项第一（AIME24 因 4096 上下文截断与 30 题粒度仅 13.33%，略低于 EDGE-GRPO 的 16.67%）。7B 上优势普遍大于 1.5B，表明容量足够时 EGPO 更能抬高性能上限。RQ2（消融，Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B，平均分）：默认 EGPO 65.97 最高；对称裁剪 C1 仅 60.02（MATH-500 66.0 vs 76.4），单向约束 C2 59.71、C3 62.08 均劣；全错组固定 w_i≡1 的 C4 62.10，说明 NSR 需与熵加权协同（Minerva 37.13 vs 34.61）；「裁剪→归一化」C5 的 MATH-500 可达 81.05 但平均 65.54 不稳定，设为非默认。RQ3（可视化）：EGPO 在正确/错误 rollout 的响应熵上分离度最大，正确样本均值 0.080、错误样本 0.129、Δ=0.048，而 GRPO/EDGE-GRPO/DAPO 仅 0.011/0.012/0.004，Base 甚至为负（-0.020），表明内在不确定性与最终答案正确性实现了对齐；另给出 EGPO 以更高熵仍得出正确解答而基线失败的定性案例。RQ4（细粒度不确定性）：区分 <think> 内的思考熵（TE）与答案侧熵（AE），答案侧熵对「预测错误」更有判别力，ROC-AUC 在 1.5B 由 0.745 提升至 0.778、7B 由 0.700 提升至 0.738；并验证熵与答案长度无强单调关系，不是长度伪相关。

Conclusion: EGPO 通过在组内用内在不确定性对优势幅度做结果感知的非对称缩放，并借助熵阻尼 NSR 从全错组中抢救学习信号，在不改动验证器与奖励定义的前提下实现了对二元 RLVR 的「元认知校准」，在多模型、多基准上取得一致且可观的提升，且正确/错误 rollout 的熵分离度最大，支撑「知道自己知道什么」的原则。局限与可讨论点：AIME24 上受上下文长度与题量离散性限制，绝对精度仍低，Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B 的 AIME24 未超过 EDGE-GRPO；表中 EGPO 在 Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B 的 MATH-500 主结果（74.00）与消融表（76.40）不一致；重归一化带来的增益不稳定；方法依赖 rollout 时的 token 对数概率与组内相对比较，对采样规模与长推理轨迹的敏感性有待进一步分析；仅在数学/QA 类可验证任务上验证，扩展到通用多模态或 agentic 任务仍待检验。

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) have emerged as a powerful paradigm for solving complex real-world tasks. In practice, these models are predominantly trained via Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), yet most existing outcome-only RLVR pipelines rely almost exclusively on a binary correctness signal and largely ignore the model's intrinsic uncertainty. We term this discrepancy the uncertainty-reward mismatch, under which high- and low-uncertainty solutions are treated equivalently, preventing the policy from "Know What You Know" and impeding the shift from optimizing for correct answers to optimizing effective reasoning paths. This limitation is especially critical in reasoning-centric tasks such as mathematics and question answering, where performance hinges on the quality of the model's internal reasoning process rather than mere memorization of final answers. To address this, we propose EGPO, a metacognitive entropy calibration framework that explicitly integrates intrinsic uncertainty into RLVR for enhancing LRMs. EGPO estimates per-sample uncertainty using a zero-overhead entropy proxy derived from token-level likelihoods and aligns it with extrinsic correctness through an asymmetric calibration mechanism that preserves correct reasoning while selectively regulating overconfident failures, thereby enabling stable and uncertainty-aware policy optimization. Moreover, EGPO recovers informative learning signals from otherwise degenerate group-based rollouts without modifying the verifier or reward definition. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed EGPO leads to substantial and consistent improvements in reasoning performance, establishing a principled path for advancing LRMs through metacognitive entropy calibration.

</details>


### [73] [Decomposing Physician Disagreement in HealthBench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22758)
*Satya Borgohain,Roy Mariathas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究对 HealthBench 医疗 AI 评测基准中的医生评分分歧做了方差分解与可解释性分析。结论是：分歧主要由单个案例的内在属性驱动，而非评分者或评分标准本身。具体而言，rubric 身份解释了 15.8% 的 met/not-met 标签方差，但仅解释 3.6–6.9% 的分歧方差；医生身份仅解释 2.4%；占 81.8% 的案例级残差无法被 HealthBench 元数据标签（z = -0.22, p = 0.83）、规范性评分语言（pseudo R² = 1.2%）、专科（0/300 Tukey 配对显著）、表层特征分诊（AUC = 0.58）或嵌入表示（AUC = 0.485）所解释。分歧与作答质量呈倒 U 型关系（AUC = 0.689），即医生在明显好/坏的输出上一致，而在边界案例上分裂。经医生验证的不确定性类别显示，可约减不确定性（信息缺失、表述歧义）使分歧几率翻倍以上（OR = 2.55, p < 10⁻²⁴），不可约不确定性（真实医学歧义）则无影响（OR = 1.01, p = 0.90），但前者也仅解释约 3% 的总方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HealthBench 等医疗 AI 基准把医生评分当作事实上的"金标准"，但其评分者间一致性（inter-rater agreement）存在明显天花板，限制了基准的效度与可解释性。既有文献多关注如何提升单个医生模型的判分准确率，而忽视了"分歧本身"的来源结构：分歧究竟来自评分标准（rubric）的性质、评分医生个人的偏好，还是被评案例本身的内在歧义？若不先定位方差驻留的位置，就无法判断应当改进评测提示与情境设计、提升标注一致性，还是接受医学任务固有的判定模糊。因此本文的动机是：（1）对 HealthBench 中的医生分歧进行系统方差分解，量化各类可观测特征（rubric 身份、医生身份、元数据、规范性语言、专科、表层特征、语义嵌入、答案质量、不确定性类型）对分歧的解释力；（2）在此基础上区分"可约减不确定性"与"不可约不确定性"，为评测基准的改进设计提供可操作的抓手。

Method: 采用多层嵌套的方差分解与"解释力竞赛"式验证设计。首先在 met/not-met 二元标签上建立混合效应模型，将总方差分解为 rubric 层、医生层与案例层成分（分别报告标签方差与分歧方差的占比），并用 z 检验/显著性检验评估各成分是否非零。随后构建多组解释变量并逐一建模其对分歧的预测力：HealthBench 提供的元数据标签（似然比/嵌套模型比较，z = -0.22, p = 0.83）、评分标准中的规范性语言强度（pseudo R² = 1.2%）、评分者的医学专科（组间比较配合 Tukey HSD，共 300 组配对，0 组显著）、由文本表层特征训练的分诊模型（分类器 AUC = 0.58）、以及基于语义嵌入的分类器（AUC = 0.485，接近随机 0.5）。再以作答完成质量为自变量检验分歧的非线性关系，观察到倒 U 型形态并以 AUC = 0.689 刻画边界区域。最后，由医生对每个分歧案例标注不确定性类别（可约减：情境信息缺失、评分标准表述歧义；不可约：真实的临床/医学歧义），通过逻辑回归计算分歧几率比（OR），并报告其对总方差的解释比例（约 3%）。整体框架强调：只有通过统计显著性门槛的解释变量才被视为真实的方差来源，其余归入残余。

Result: （1）方差结构：rubric 身份解释 15.8% 的 met/not-met 标签方差，但只解释 3.6–6.9% 的分歧方差；医生身份仅贡献 2.4%；案例级残差占 81.8%，是分歧的主导成分。（2）可观测特征几乎无法约减该残差：元数据标签无显著贡献（z = -0.22, p = 0.83）；规范性 rubric 语言解释率仅 1.2%（pseudo R²）；专科差异 300 组 Tukey 配对中 0 组显著；表层特征分诊 AUC = 0.58，嵌入模型 AUC = 0.485（等同随机）。（3）分歧与作答质量呈倒 U 型关系（AUC = 0.689），表明医生在明显优质或明显劣质的输出上高度一致，仅在"边界"答案上出现分裂。（4）不确定性类别的分离效应显著：可约减不确定性使分歧几率增加逾一倍（OR = 2.55, p < 10⁻²⁴），不可约不确定性无效应（OR = 1.01, p = 0.90）；即便可约减不确定性也仅解释约 3% 的总方差，说明其虽方向明确但覆盖面有限。

Conclusion: 医疗 AI 评测中的一致性上限在很大程度上是结构性的：绝大部分分歧（81.8%）源自案例层面的内在判定模糊，无法通过现有的元数据、评分标准措辞、专科或语义表征来预测或消除，单纯"提要求人更一致"或"更换标注者"的收益有限（医生身份仅 2.4%）。关键的实践启示来自可约减/不可约不确定性的解耦——在缺乏上下文或评分标准表述含糊之处补齐信息能显著降低分歧（OR = 2.55），而对真实存在的临床歧义则无能为力（OR = 1.01）；因此评测设计改进应聚焦于场景与 rubric 的信息完备性与措辞无歧义化，而非试图消除所有分歧。同时倒 U 型结果提示：区分"清晰好/清晰坏"与"边界案例"、并在基准中显式标出边界子集，有助于提高评测信号的判别效率。总体而言，剩余的可提升空间虽小但方向明确，HealthBench 一类基准应以结构化标注不确定性来源来替代追求完美一致性的思路。

Abstract: We decompose physician disagreement in the HealthBench medical AI evaluation dataset to understand where variance resides and what observable features can explain it. Rubric identity accounts for 15.8% of met/not-met label variance but only 3.6-6.9% of disagreement variance; physician identity accounts for just 2.4%. The dominant 81.8% case-level residual is not reduced by HealthBench's metadata labels (z = -0.22, p = 0.83), normative rubric language (pseudo R^2 = 1.2%), medical specialty (0/300 Tukey pairs significant), surface-feature triage (AUC = 0.58), or embeddings (AUC = 0.485). Disagreement follows an inverted-U with completion quality (AUC = 0.689), confirming physicians agree on clearly good or bad outputs but split on borderline cases. Physician-validated uncertainty categories reveal that reducible uncertainty (missing context, ambiguous phrasing) more than doubles disagreement odds (OR = 2.55, p < 10^(-24)), while irreducible uncertainty (genuine medical ambiguity) has no effect (OR = 1.01, p = 0.90), though even the former explains only ~3% of total variance. The agreement ceiling in medical AI evaluation is thus largely structural, but the reducible/irreducible dissociation suggests that closing information gaps in evaluation scenarios could lower disagreement where inherent clinical ambiguity does not, pointing toward actionable evaluation design improvements.

</details>


### [74] [AMA-Bench: Evaluating Long-Horizon Memory for Agentic Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22769)
*Yujie Zhao,Boqin Yuan,Junbo Huang,Haocheng Yuan,Zhongming Yu,Haozhou Xu,Lanxiang Hu,Abhilash Shankarampeta,Zimeng Huang,Wentao Ni,Yuandong Tian,Jishen Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AMA-Bench 是首个面向智能体（agent）应用长时程记忆的评测基准，包含真实世界轨迹（6 个领域、2,496 条 QA）与可扩展至任意长度（8K–128K）的合成轨迹（1,200 条 QA）；配套提出的 AMA-Agent 通过因果图（Causality Graph）与工具增强检索（Tool-Augmented Retrieval）在该基准上取得 57.22% 平均准确率，比最强基线高 11.16 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆基准（LoCoMo、LongMemEval、MemoryAgentBench、MemoryBench、RealTalk 等）以对话式人机交互为主，天然语言冗余多、缺乏因果结构；而真实智能体记忆是「状态—动作—观察—工具输出」的机器生成轨迹流，呈现三大差异：(1) 表示类型多样（ASCII 表、JSON、代码块、HTML 等符号化表示）；(2) 具有因果性（每个动作诱导潜在环境状态转移并约束后续观察）；(3) 目标信息密集（无寒暄等冗余）。缺少针对长时程 agentic 任务的记忆评测，导致记忆系统的真实瓶颈无法被诊断。此外尚不清楚性能瓶颈来自骨干模型容量还是记忆架构设计本身。

Method: 问题形式化：将 agent-环境交互建模为 POMDP (S, A, O, P, r)，轨迹 h_t=(x,a_1,o_1,…,o_t)；记忆系统被统一拆为 Build（历史→记忆态 m_t）与 Retrieve（m_t,q→上下文 c_t）两阶段。能力分类：三大机制（检索/演化/凝缩）对应四类能力——A. 召回（时序与序列信息）、B. 因果推断（动作前置条件与状态依赖）、C. 状态更新（显式与隐式状态追踪）、D. 状态抽象（去冗余与精确凝缩）。

基准构建：① 真实子集——从 Embodied AI (ALFWorld/ALFRED)、Gaming (BALROG/LMGame-Bench)、Web (WebArena)、软件工程 (SWE-bench/OpenHands)、开放世界工具 (GAIA/Co-Sight)、Text2SQL (Spider 2.0/Spider2-Agent) 采集 208 条长轨迹（平均 73.3 轮、57.5K tokens，最大 996K），每条由研究生级标注者撰写 12 条覆盖四类的 QA，并交叉复核；② 合成子集——以难度向量 φ 与随机种子程序化合成可执行环境（BabyAI、TextWorld），可机器验证的状态转移，支持动作随机性 ε（鲁棒性）与观察冗长度 γ（表示粒度）扰动；基于最优策略 π* 生成轨迹、由后端状态变量自动生成 QA，并实现「needle」协议（needle = 包含全部证据的最小轮次 ID 集合），可在 8K/16K/32K/64K/128K 五档等量采样。评测采用离线 per-QA 协议（一次性摄入完整轨迹后固定记忆态作答），指标为 LLM-as-judge（Qwen3-32B）判定的 Accuracy 与 F1，并提供人工校验（92.67% 一致）与四个异构 judge 的交叉一致性。

AMA-Agent：(A) 因果图构建——对每个相邻轮 (o_{t-1}, a_t, o_t) 做 LLM 语义抽象，抽取环境/对象状态与潜在因果依赖，实例化为有向因果边与无向关联边，节点映射到隐式嵌入空间（Qwen3-4B-Embedding）；分段独立抽象使局部误差不级联。(B) 工具增强检索——先按嵌入相似度取 top-K（K=5）并自评充分性；不足时路由至「图节点搜索」（受控深度邻域遍历，聚合多跳与因果关系）或「关键词搜索」（由 LLM 编写并执行脚本做精确匹配与统计聚合）；实测图工具仅 10.0%、关键词工具 23.5% 的情形被调用。

Result: ① 模型层面：GPT 5.2 最高但仅 72.26% 平均准确率（Recall 0.774 / Causal 0.805 / State Updating 0.656 / Abstraction 0.604），说明前沿模型尚未掌握轨迹型记忆。② 记忆系统普遍不及长上下文基线；8B→32B 扩模平均仅 +0.038，而更换记忆架构带来高达 0.45 的分数方差，说明记忆设计是主要瓶颈。③ Needle 消融定位两类损失：MemoryBank 在构建阶段骤降 41.3%（面向冗余自然语言的压缩丢失密集状态与因果信息），HippoRAG2 端到端下降 43.2%（相似度检索不可靠）。④ AMA-Agent 在四类能力上全面领先：0.6238/0.6145/0.5305/0.4719，平均 0.5722，超过 HippoRAG2 (0.4480)、MemoRAG (0.4606)、EMem (0.4610) 及 HiMem（超 27 个百分点）。⑤ 合成子集与真实子集排名高度相关（贴近对角线），可作高保真代理；长上下文在 32K 后显著衰减，AMA-Agent 在 128K 仍保持稳定。⑥ 下游有效性：TextWorld/Spider2 上 QA 准确率与端到端成功率相关系数达 0.96/0.98，AMA-Agent 两项指标全面领先（TW E2E 51.5、Sp2 E2E 26.2）。⑦ 消融：去掉因果图降至 0.43（−24.6%），去掉工具增强检索降至 0.44（−22.8%），两模块均必要。⑧ 效率：AMA-Agent 端到端 8.50s/轨迹，快于 HippoRAG2/Mem1/SimpleMem 且更准；QA 评测比完整 rollout 快约 10^3 倍。⑨ 泛化性：跨骨干（Qwen3-8B、GPT-5-mini）排序保持一致；在对话式 LoCoMo 上仍达 76.20%，超过 HippoRAG 4.1 个百分点。

Conclusion: AMA-Bench 弥合了自然语言中心评测与机器生成、因果化的真实 agent 轨迹之间的鸿沟，表明记忆架构（而非骨干规模）是长时程 agent 记忆性能的首要决定因素，现有有损压缩与相似度检索难以承载密集目标信息。为此提出 AMA-Agent，以因果图保留客观信息与显式因果依赖、以工具增强检索补足相似度检索的信息抽取短板，显著超越 SOTA 基线。局限：仅评估 episode 内记忆，尚未覆盖跨任务/终身学习场景。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are deployed as autonomous agents in increasingly complex applications, where enabling long-horizon memory is critical for achieving strong performance. However, a significant gap exists between practical applications and current evaluation standards for agent memory: existing benchmarks primarily focus on dialogue-centric, human-agent interactions. In reality, agent memory consists of a continuous stream of agent-environment interactions that are primarily composed of machine-generated representations. To bridge this gap, we introduce AMA-Bench (Agent Memory with Any length), which evaluates long-horizon memory for LLMs in real agentic applications. It features two key components: (1) a set of real-world agentic trajectories across representative agentic applications, paired with expert-curated QA, and (2) a set of synthetic agentic trajectories that scale to arbitrary horizons, paired with rule-based QA. Our comprehensive study shows that existing memory systems underperform on AMA-Bench primarily because they lack causality and objective information and are constrained by the lossy nature of similarity-based retrieval employed by many memory systems. To address these limitations, we propose AMA-Agent, an effective memory system featuring a causality graph and tool-augmented retrieval. Our results demonstrate that AMA-Agent achieves 57.22% average accuracy on AMA-Bench, surpassing the strongest memory system baselines by 11.16%.

</details>


### [75] [MiroFlow: Towards High-Performance and Robust Open-Source Agent Framework for General Deep Research Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22808)
*Shiqian Su,Sen Xing,Xuan Dong,Muyan Zhong,Bin Wang,Xizhou Zhu,Yuntao Chen,Wenhai Wang,Yue Deng,Pengxiang Zhu,Ziyuan Liu,Tiantong Li,Jiaheng Yu,Zhe Chen,Lidong Bing,Jifeng Dai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MiroFlow 是清华大学与 MiroMind AI 联合推出的开源深度研究智能体框架，采用「控制层—智能体层—基础层」三级分层架构，核心是 Agent Graph（有向图编排，支持并行/交织执行）、可选的 Heavy-Reasoning 模式（集成策略+生成器—验证器迭代策略）与 Robust Workflow（消息规范化、重试—回退—重放、故障隔离）。在 GAIA、BrowseComp-EN/ZH、HLE、xBench-DeepSearch、FutureX 六个基准上以同一套配置、无任务定制取得 SOTA：GPT-5 骨干平均 73.5（MiroThinker-72B 平均 77.8），FutureX 达 42.5（远超 GPT-5-Thinking-Heavy 25.6、ChatGPT-Agent 21.9）。消融显示消息规范化与重试机制同时提升准确率并降低方差，I/O 处理中输出后处理贡献最大，代码已开源，定位为深研社区可复现、可比较的统一基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 独立运行的 LLM 在需要外部工具交互与动态环境的复杂真实任务上已明显趋于饱和，虽已出现大量开源智能体框架，但仍存在三大痛点：(1) 僵化性——多为硬编码的单用途流水线，难以泛化到异构任务；(2) 不稳定性——长推理链、随机采样、工具调用失败与检索结果随机性导致分数波动大、结果难以复现，且模型常误读工具/网络错误而放弃工具甚至幻觉作答；(3) 高成本——严重依赖昂贵的商业 API，阻碍开放研究与规模化。因此需要一个灵活可编排、执行稳健可复现、且以开源组件为主的通用深度研究智能体基线。

Method: MiroFlow 采用三层架构：基础层（LLM 后端、MCP 工具集、通用 I/O 处理器）、智能体层（由描述/提示/子智能体/工具/I/O 处理器构成的独立节点，以结构化消息通信）、控制层（把节点组装成 Agent Graph，负责编排、日志与检查点、预算与重推理模式）。Agent Graph 采用「先声明后定义」的有向图方式，主智能体为入口形成层次拓扑，边定义依赖关系，支持并行与交织执行，避免链式/树式的固定顺序限制。Heavy-Reasoning Mode 是控制层的元级执行策略，含两种策略：集成策略（并行运行同构/异构多智能体，按多数或加权投票进行质量感知聚合）与验证策略（生成器—验证器迭代循环，满足判据或达轮数上限即早停），由节点按提示级判据按需激活，受智能体数/验证轮数/墙钟时间预算约束，仅被激活的子图进入重推理，其余保持轻量。Robust Workflow 包含：消息规范化（先列计划再输出满足类型/单位/引用的结构化答案；将用户任务改写为明确目标、补全缺失约束并标注歧义；输出写入 final answer/evidence/warnings 等结构化字段）；重试机制（对所有模型与工具调用执行「重试—回退—重放」，含重试次数与超时，失败后切换冗余工具而不改变智能体逻辑）；故障隔离（错误被捕获并转为带类型的摘要工件上报，避免跨组件级联，向 LLM 传递明确可解释的反馈，恢复决策局部化可回滚）。实验默认工具集含 o3 推理工具、Jina+Serper 网页搜索、Claude Vision 图像问答、Gemini 2.5 Pro 视频问答、MarkItDown 文档解析、GPT-4o 音频转写、E2B 代码沙箱；骨干为 GPT-5、Claude 3.7 Sonnet 与开源 MiroThinker。遵循各基准官方协议与提示，GAIA-Val-Text（103 题）用 LLM-as-a-judge，除特别说明均为 avg@3。

Result: 1) SOTA 与通用性：GPT-5 配置下 GAIA-Val 71.90、GAIA-Test 79.90（重推理 81.1*）、BrowseComp-EN 63.4、BrowseComp-ZH 59.7、HLE 59.7、HLE-Text 35.5、xBench-DS 40.0、平均 73.5；MiroThinker-v1.0-72B 在 GAIA-Test 81.9、BrowseComp 47.1、HLE 55.6、xBench-DS 37.7、平均 77.8（GAIA/HLE 多模态为 N/A）；Claude-3.7 Sonnet 配置平均 72.0。所有结果均出自同一统一配置、无任务定制，覆盖面与泛化性优于多数仅支持单一基准的系统。2) FutureX 榜单 42.5，自 2025 年 9 月起持续居首，约为独立 GPT-5 的两倍，显著高于 GPT-5-Thinking-Heavy 25.6、ChatGPT-Agent 21.9、EventDeepResearch-V2 18.7。3) 稳健性消融（GAIA，GPT-5）：去掉消息规范化降至 68.5（标准差 1.21→2.43），去掉重试机制降至 69.0（标准差升至 1.70），证明二者同时提升准确率与稳定性。4) 重推理模式：集成策略随规模提升，GPT-5×4×4（异质提示）达 75.0，×4×4 同提示 74.6，×2×2+Claude 73.0，验证策略 10 轮 73.0，均优于默认 71.9。5) 单/多智能体：多智能体在 BrowseComp-200（68.3 vs 63.9）与 HLE-200（42.0 vs 40.6）更优，但单智能体在 GAIA-Val 更好（74.8 vs 71.9），归因于 GAIA 强序列结构下多智能体存在信息丢失与错误传播风险。6) 最大轮数与上下文长度：轮数增加先升后饱和，多智能体收敛更快；上下文从 8k 增至 24k–48k 提升显著，延伸至 400k 收益边际，难题（L3）最依赖长上下文。7) 开源工具集（Qwen2.5-VL-72B、Qwen3-235B-A22B-thinking、whisper-large-v3-turbo）可取得接近默认工具的表现（GAIA-Val 70.3 vs 71.9，GAIA-Val-Text 79.0 vs 79.9）。8) I/O 处理消融：无处理 59.60（方差 3.34），仅输出 73.94（方差 2.18），输入+输出 71.9（方差 1.21），说明输出后处理是主要收益来源，输入处理主要起稳定作用。9) 可复现性审视：作者复现 OWL（69.7→53.9）与 AgentOrchestra（82.4→54.6）均显著低于其原文报告，指出这些框架鲁棒性/可复现性不足。

Conclusion: MiroFlow 通过分层智能体架构、Agent Graph、稳健工作流与可选重推理模式的组合，在多个异构深度研究基准上以统一配置取得可复现的 SOTA 结果，且支持开源模型与开源工具的近乎无差别部署，显著降低了研究与使用成本。作者主张其可作为深研社区易获取、可复现、可比较的统一基线，并为智能体系统的设计（编排灵活性、错误语义化、预算化推理扩展、上下文与轮数配置）提供了较系统的实证依据。需注意的边界：GAIA-Val-Text 仅 103 题且采用 LLM-as-a-judge 评分；带 * 的增益来自多智能体结果融合；多智能体在强序列型任务（GAIA）上反而退化；部分对比基线难以复现，横向排名的可比性需谨慎解读；重推理模式带来的提升伴随显著算力与成本增长。

Abstract: Despite the remarkable progress of large language models (LLMs), the capabilities of standalone LLMs have begun to plateau when tackling real-world, complex tasks that require interaction with external tools and dynamic environments. Although recent agent frameworks aim to enhance model autonomy through tool integration and external interaction, they still suffer from naive workflows, unstable performance, limited support across diverse benchmarks and tasks, and heavy reliance on costly commercial APIs. In this work, we propose a high-performance and robust open-source agent framework, termed MiroFlow, which incorporates an agent graph for flexible orchestration, an optional deep reasoning mode to enhance performance, and a robust workflow execution to ensure stable and reproducible performance. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MiroFlow consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple agent benchmarks, including GAIA, BrowseComp-EN/ZH, HLE, xBench-DeepSearch, and notably FutureX. We hope it could serve as an easily accessible, reproducible, and comparable baseline for the deep research community.

</details>


### [76] [When Should an AI Act? A Human-Centered Model of Scene, Context, and Behavior for Agentic AI Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22814)
*Soyoung Jung,Daehoo Yoon,Sung Gyu Koh,Young Hwan Kim,Yehan Ahn,Sung Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个"行为解释"概念模型，将智能体行为视为由 Scene（可观测情境）、Context（用户建构的意义）与 Human Behavior Factors（行为倾向决定因素）整合而成的解释性结果，并据此推导出五项智能体设计原则：行为对齐、情境敏感性、时间适切性、动机校准与自主性保留，为具备情境敏感性与判断力的主动式代理式 AI 提供设计基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理式 AI（Agentic AI）越来越多地依据环境上下文数据推断用户处境并进行主动干预，但往往因缺乏关于"何时行动、为何行动、是否行动"的原则性判断机制而失败。现有研究多聚焦技术能力（感知、规划、工具调用），缺少一套区分"可观测事实"与"对用户有意义的意义"的理论框架，导致主动干预时机不当、强度失当或越权行动。本文跨越人文学科、社会科学、HCI 与工程视角，试图补足这一理论缺口。

Method: 作者构建一个多学科整合的概念模型：以 Scene（可观测情境，如环境数据、设备状态、交互历史）描述"发生了什么"，以 Context（用户建构的意义，如任务意图、个人目标、情境约束）描述"这对用户意味着什么"，以 Human Behavior Factors（认知、动机、情感、社会与生理等决定行为可能性的因素）描述"用户可能如何行动"。三者整合形成行为的解释性输出，而非单纯的刺激—反应。模型强调同一 Scene 可因 Context 与行为因素差异而产生不同的行为意义与结果。随后将该视角转化为设计可操作层，提取出五项智能体设计原则：行为对齐（behavioral alignment）、情境敏感性（contextual sensitivity）、时间适切性（temporal appropriateness）、动机校准（motivational calibration）、自主性保留（agency preservation），并用它们共同刻画干预的深度、时机、强度与克制程度（restraint）。

Result: 产出一套"情境—意义—行为"的三元概念模型及其配套的五项智能体设计原则，形成可用于刻画与评价主动式 AI 干预决策（是否行动、何时行动、行动多强、何时克制）的统一分析语言。模型可解释同一情境在不同用户或不同语境下导致不同行为结果的差异性来源；原则集为系统设计、行为评估与交互流程设计提供可迁移的规范性依据。

Conclusion: 该模型与五项原则共同为设计"具备情境敏感性与判断力"的代理式 AI 系统奠定理论基础，强调主动干预应建立在对情境、用户意义与行为动因的联合解释之上，并需在干预强度与用户自主权之间保持平衡；作为概念性工作，作者亦指出该框架有待后续在具体系统与实证场景中的检验、细化与操作化。

Abstract: Agentic AI increasingly intervenes proactively by inferring users' situations from contextual data yet often fails for lack of principled judgment about when, why, and whether to act. We address this gap by proposing a conceptual model that reframes behavior as an interpretive outcome integrating Scene (observable situation), Context (user-constructed meaning), and Human Behavior Factors (determinants shaping behavioral likelihood). Grounded in multidisciplinary perspectives across the humanities, social sciences, HCI, and engineering, the model separates what is observable from what is meaningful to the user and explains how the same scene can yield different behavioral meanings and outcomes. To translate this lens into design action, we derive five agent design principles (behavioral alignment, contextual sensitivity, temporal appropriateness, motivational calibration, and agency preservation) that guide intervention depth, timing, intensity, and restraint. Together, the model and principles provide a foundation for designing agentic AI systems that act with contextual sensitivity and judgment in interactions.

</details>


### [77] [FlexMS is a flexible framework for benchmarking deep learning-based mass spectrum prediction tools in metabolomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22822)
*Yunhua Zhong,Yixuan Tang,Yifan Li,Jie Yang,Pan Liu,Jun Xia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FlexMS 是一个模块化、公开数据驱动的 MS/MS 谱预测基准框架，将分子编码器、元数据条件化、谱预测头与下游检索任务统一到同一评测协议下，系统比较了 11 种分子表征与 3 种预测头的组合，揭示出「GFv2+MassFormer 最强但代价高、GIN/描述符 MLP 更适合资源受限场景」等可操作结论，并强调 scaffold 划分、跨域迁移与检索任务对模型排序的显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 串联质谱（MS/MS）在小分子鉴定、药物发现与代谢组学中至关重要，但深度学习谱预测领域缺乏统一的评测协议：新方法常以「临时拼装」方式构建，导致模型族系、元数据假设、划分语义、预处理流程与评价指标相互纠缠，性能增益无法归因于架构本身；同时现有评测通常只覆盖少数精选数据集与单一任务，缺乏对数据稀疏性、计算预算、跨域偏移等现实约束的诊断，也无法回答「在算力/数据受限时该选哪类模型」这一实际部署问题。因此需要模块化、可复现且面向诊断的基准设施。

Method: 提出 FlexMS 模块化基准框架：①Featurizer（Morgan/MACCS/物理化学描述符/Daylight/PubChem/AtomPair/ErG/拼接描述符、One-hot SMILES、ESPF-BPE、ChemBERTa、3D 与图特征）将原始分子转为数学表示；②Metadata Encoder 标准化 ACE/NCE（缺失指示+标准化）、离散化前体 m/z、仪器类型与电离模式 one-hot 并拼接为上下文向量；③Embedder（MLP 类、1D-CNN、Transformer/DeepPurpose/ChemBERTa/MoleBERT、GNN 类 GCN/GAT/GIN/AttentiveFP/GraphormerV2）负责学习潜表示；④Predictor（MassFormer 双向门控、NEIMS 轻量 MLP、MolMS 残差编码器-解码器）将表示映射为分箱（1–10 Da）强度分布的多标签回归输出。数据集覆盖 GNPS（>322k 谱/16k 分子）、MassBank（>62k/4k）、MassSpecGym（231k/29k）、NPLIB1（~8k/7k）以及 CASMI 2016/2022 检索挑战；划分采用官方划分与 Bemis–Murcko 骨架划分（80/10/10）配对；诊断包括基于 Morgan 指纹 Tanimoto 与双样本 KS 检验、谱熵分析、分箱分辨率 1–10 Da 消融、学习率 1e-3 vs 1e-4、数据稀疏（25/50/100%）、预训练（MoleBERT）对照、离子化模式（[M+H]+ vs [M+Na]+）与仪器（Orbitrap vs QTOF）跨域迁移、计算开销（时间/峰值显存）；指标含 Cosine Similarity、Jensen–Shannon Similarity、Spectral Coverage、Rank/NormRank、Top-K 与 Top-p% 准确率，统计检验采用 Friedman + Wilcoxon-Holm 临界差异图。训练统一 AdamW、base LR 1e-4、batch 512、最多 100 epoch 早停、seed 42、损失为 1−mean(cosine_sim)。

Result: （1）数据集诊断：骨架划分产生显著分布偏移（GNPS/MassBank 训练-测试的 log KS p 值分别约 −45.7 与 −274，对应 Tanimoto 均值几乎相同），谱熵分析独立验证该结论（骨架划分 KS 统计量为随机划分的 4.3–7.1 倍）；NPLIB1 谱熵最高（H≈2.8）反映天然产物碎裂复杂性。（2）架构对比（MassSpecGym）：GraphormerV2 在所有预测头与分辨率下均最优（MassFormer+GFv2：Cos 0.357、JS 0.516），预测头排序为 MassFormer > NEIMS > MolMS；存在强协同效应——MolMS 搭配 GNN 编码器、NEIMS 搭配 MLP 编码器更佳。（3）计算开销：GFv2 单次前向时间最高（MassFormer 约 123.5 s/批、峰值显存约 24.4 GB），Transformer/ChemBERTa 次之，而 GIN、All-Descriptor MLP 性价比更佳；在 GNPS 上训练 GFv2 即使 A100 也需数天，故资源受限场景推荐 GIN 或 All-Descriptors MLP。（4）数据稀疏：25% 划分下复杂归纳偏置模型（深度 GNN/Transformer）严重退化，CNN、指纹 MLP 与预训练骨干保持稳健；数据增至 100% 时 GNN 才能发挥结构偏置优势。（5）学习率：异质性高的 MassBank/MassSpecGym 偏好 1e-4，精细整理的 NPLIB1 偏好 1e-3；整体学习率影响小于架构影响。（6）预训练：MoleBERT 预训练在随机划分上增益有限，但在骨架划分上增益更稳定且更大（如 NPLIB1/MassFormer 0.5939 vs 0.5337），并加快微调收敛。（7）跨域迁移呈不对称性：源域谱量少但迁移效果更好——[M+Na]+（15%）→[M+H]+（85%）优于反向，Orbitrap（75%）→QTOF 优于反向，提示域覆盖应按信息含量与可迁移性而非样本量评估；同域内 [M+H]+ 优于 [M+Na]+、QTOF 优于 Orbitrap。（8）检索基准：重建指标与检索排名并非完全线性；GFv2 在 CASMI 2016 归一化排名最优（0.1022，Recall@10 0.6667）；GCN 出现「检索强、重建弱」的悖论，源于 D^-1/2AD^-1/2 聚合的低通平滑抑制局部异质性，却提供稳定的判别包络；CASMI 2022 因候选池更大（平均约 4849 vs 1251）性能大幅下降（Recall@10 由约 60% 降至不足 19%），但模型相对排序保持一致，GFv2 优势收窄。（9）分辨率效应：粗化分箱普遍提升检索准确率（1→2 Da 增益最大），图模型呈稳定单调改善，序列/预训练语言表征轨迹混乱、非单调。（10）检索任务放大优化不稳定：GFv2 在 LR 1e-3 下 Top-10 准确率崩塌至约 0.06，降至 1e-4 恢复至 0.60–0.66。

Conclusion: FlexMS 将 MS/MS 谱预测从「排行榜竞赛」重构为「模块化基础设施问题」，贡献在于可复现的公开评测生态、统一的跨数据集协议（官方/骨架划分、元数据条件化、共享相似度指标）以及难度感知诊断（分子相似度 + 谱熵）三者结合。核心结论是模型精度高度依赖评测协议：GFv2+MassFormer 在统一协议下最强但计算开销巨大，数据/算力受限时 GIN 或描述符 MLP 是更优折中，模型选择应依据数据规模、数据质量（学习率）、域覆盖与下游目标（重建 vs 检索）而定，而非单一平均分。局限在于未覆盖全部最新 SOTA 方法与先进训练技巧，尚未处理峰列表/公式感知等变长输出形式，跨域分析仅限离子类型与仪器（碰撞能差异等未系统覆盖），效率评估也需标准化预算报告；未来方向包括扩展到峰值列表预测、更多域偏移协议、预算感知评测与更广数据集覆盖。

Abstract: The identification and property prediction of chemical molecules is of central importance in the advancement of drug discovery and material science, where the tandem mass spectrometry technology gives valuable fragmentation cues in the form of mass-to-charge ratio peaks. However, the lack of experimental spectra hinders the attachment of each molecular identification, and thus urges the establishment of prediction approaches for computational models. Deep learning models appear promising for predicting molecular structure spectra, but overall assessment remains challenging as a result of the heterogeneity in methods and the lack of well-defined benchmarks. To address this, our contribution is the creation of benchmark framework FlexMS for constructing and evaluating diverse model architectures in mass spectrum prediction. With its easy-to-use flexibility, FlexMS supports the dynamic construction of numerous distinct combinations of model architectures, while assessing their performance on preprocessed public datasets using different metrics. In this paper, we provide insights into factors influencing performance, including the structural diversity of datasets, hyperparameters like learning rate and data sparsity, pretraining effects, metadata ablation settings and cross-domain transfer learning analysis. This provides practical guidance in choosing suitable models. Moreover, retrieval benchmarks simulate practical identification scenarios and score potential matches based on predicted spectra.

</details>


### [78] [DeepPresenter: Environment-Grounded Reflection for Agentic Presentation Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22839)
*Hao Zheng,Guozhao Mo,Xinru Yan,Qianhao Yuan,Wenkai Zhang,Xuanang Chen,Yaojie Lu,Hongyu Lin,Xianpei Han,Le Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DeepPresenter 是一个面向演示文稿生成的双智能体（Researcher + Presenter）框架，其核心创新是"环境落地式反思"（environment-grounded reflection）：通过 inspect 工具把渲染后的幻灯片像素和稿件诊断结果暴露给智能体，使其在"观察—反思—修订"闭环中修复元素重叠、文字截断、对比度过低等仅在渲染后可见的缺陷，而非依赖推理轨迹等内部信号的盲反思。作者同时构建了 1,152 条带可验证约束的任务集（PersonaHub/arXiv/FinePDFs-Edu），用"外在验证"（独立 critic 隔离式审查）合成轨迹并三阶段过滤，蒸馏出 9B 的 DeepPresenter-9B。评测上 Gemini-3-Pro 版平均 4.44（超过商业系统 Gamma 的 4.36 与最佳开源基线 3.92），多样性 0.79 远超模板方法；DeepPresenter-9B 得 4.19，以更低成本逼近 GPT-5 的 4.22。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 演示文稿生成需要深度内容研究、连贯视觉设计与基于观察的迭代优化，但现有智能体方案存在两类缺陷：(1) 依赖预定义工作流与内容无关的固定模板，难以适配多样用户意图，导致文字堆砌、研究深度不足、视觉风格与叙事脱节；(2) 基于内部信号（代码、推理轨迹）的自省式反思无法发现渲染后才暴露的缺陷，如元素重叠、文字截断、布局破损、破图。根因被归纳为"状态错配"：智能体操作的是 HTML/Markdown 等中间表示，用户感知的是渲染结果，二者处于不同观察空间。因此需要一种让反思直接锚定在可感知成品状态上的机制。

Method: **1. 任务形式化**：将演示生成建模为交互式智能体轨迹 τ={（r_t, a_t, o_t）}，环境 E 含工具库 T 与文件系统 F，并分解为 τ^R（Researcher）∘ τ^P（Presenter）两个阶段，通过 F 中持久化的结构化稿件 M 通信。

**2. 双智能体协作**（共享同一 backbone）：Researcher 自主规划探索深度（技术报告需调研相关工作，通俗报告重在案例与配图），检索/合成素材并编译为按叙事流组织、含本地图片资源的 Markdown 稿件；Presenter 不填模板，而是先制定全局设计计划（配色、字体、栅格），再逐页生成独立 HTML 文件，实现"内容驱动设计"（如可持续主题用大地色系、学术教程用极简布局）。

**3. 环境落地式反思**：引入 inspect 作为显式观察接口。inspect_slide 用无头浏览器把 HTML 渲染成像素图像返回视觉上下文，暴露溢出、重叠、低对比度等缺陷；inspect_manuscript 返回稿件与文件系统的结构化诊断（页数、语言一致性、图片资产存在性、外链未下载、alt 缺失、重复用图）。智能体用 think 基于观察到的缺陷规划定向修改，形成与用户感知对齐的"观察—反思—修订"闭环。

**4. DeepPresenter-9B 训练流水线**（图3）：Query Construction 用 GLM-4.6/Qwen3-235B 从 PersonaHub 人设合成个性化请求，arXiv 与 FinePDFs-Edu 构造基于给定文档的任务，并随机附加页数、语言、宽高比（16:9 / 4:3 / A1 海报）等可验证约束，共 1,152 条（1,024 训练 / 128 评测），并做语言过滤、语义去重与 LLM 质控；Extrinsic Verification 由独立 critic 在隔离上下文中基于观察与中间产物输出缺陷与可执行建议的推理轨迹（如"对比度过低→调整文字颜色"），以 think 调用形式追加进 agent 上下文，破解自验证偏差；Trajectory Filtering 三阶段：规则校验约束合规 → GLM-4.6 评一致性（剔除反思—动作不一致者）→ GLM-4.6V 评输出质量（剔除重叠、破图等严重缺陷）。802 条合格轨迹用 MS-SWIFT 对 GLM-4.6V-Flash 微调（bs=32、lr=1e-5、5 epoch、8×A800 约 80 GPU 小时）。

**5. 评测协议**：Constraint 为规则化校验的约束满足率（页数/语言/宽高比）；Content 与 Style 采用 PPTAgent 的 MLLM 评测框架、以 GPT-5 为评审；Diversity 用 DINOv2 特征相似度矩阵特征值熵的 Vendi Score；Avg 为前三项 0–5 均值。基线为商业系统 Gamma 与开源框架 PPTAgent、KCTV，后端含 GPT-5、Gemini-3-Pro、Claude-Sonnet-4.5、GLM-4.6 等。工具集分 Retrieve / File / Reason / Control / Synthesis 五类共约 30 个动作。

Result: 主结果（128 条留出集）：DeepPresenter+Gemini-3-Pro 平均 4.44（约束 4.70 / 内容 4.25 / 风格 4.37），超过最佳开源基线 KCTV+Claude-Sonnet-4.5（3.92）13.3%，也超过商业系统 Gamma（4.36）；多样性 0.79 较模板基线（0.17–0.35）与 Gamma（0.52）成倍领先，同时保持高约束合规。其余后端：Claude-Sonnet-4.5 4.41、GPT-5 4.22、GLM-4.6V 3.90、GLM-4.6V-Flash 3.82。DeepPresenter-9B 平均 4.19，优于所有开源基线并逼近 GPT-5 的 4.22，成本远低（按 OpenRouter 基座模型 token 单价估算），并推进了性价比 Pareto 前沿。

消融（Gemini-3-Pro）：去掉环境落地反思 4.44→4.32，去掉双智能体 4.44→4.04；DeepPresenter-9B 去掉反思 4.19→3.82、去掉双智能体 4.19→3.23、去掉轨迹过滤 4.19→4.03，说明反思与小模型受益更明显。外在验证实验（同一 300 条轨迹）：仅微调 +0.12，叠加外在验证 +0.20（增益提升 67%）。缺陷统计上外在验证发现数量系统性更高（幻灯片 layout 308 vs 212、render 101 vs 43），印证自验证的合理化倾向。

失败分析：过滤前轨迹中质量问题占 43.0%、环境失败 32.3%（上下文溢出与基础设施中断）、约束违规 13.5%、一致性错误 11.2%。领域分解：PersonaHub 内容/风格 4.49 但约束 4.38（约束更复杂）；arXiv 约束 4.91 但内容 3.84、风格 4.13（学术正式性限制视觉多样性）；FinePDF 综合 4.51。工具使用分析显示 Researcher 重 Retrieve、Presenter 重 File/Reason，双智能体分工得到验证。人工评测（2 名研究生、32 份样本）与自动评测排序趋势一致，缓解 LLM-as-judge 循环性问题。局限：多步工具 rollout 成本高、对环境不稳定敏感；外在验证仅用于训练阶段，推理期因可能引入反思—动作不一致与额外开销而未采用。

Conclusion: DeepPresenter 通过"自主规划 + 双智能体协作 + 环境落地式反思"将演示文稿生成从模板化流水线转为可感知、可自我纠错的开放生成过程，验证了把反思锚定在渲染后成品状态（而非内部推理信号）是修复视觉缺陷的关键，显著提升内容深度、风格契合度与视觉多样性，并使框架能充分利用更强模型容量（3.82→4.44）而基线框架性能平坦。以 802 条经"外在验证+三阶段过滤"合成的轨迹蒸馏出的 9B 小模型，以显著更低成本达到接近 GPT-5 的水平，说明少量高质量轨迹即可习得智能体行为。作者指出后续方向为在推理阶段引入外部 critic 以进一步缓解自验证偏差。

Abstract: Presentation generation requires deep content research, coherent visual design, and iterative refinement based on observation. However, existing presentation agents often rely on predefined workflows and fixed templates. To address this, we present DeepPresenter, an agentic framework that adapts to diverse user intents, enables effective feedback-driven refinement, and generalizes beyond a scripted pipeline. Specifically, DeepPresenter autonomously plans, renders, and revises intermediate slide artifacts to support long-horizon refinement with environmental observations. Furthermore, rather than relying on self-reflection over internal signals (e.g., reasoning traces), our environment-grounded reflection conditions the generation process on perceptual artifact states (e.g., rendered slides), enabling the system to identify and correct presentation-specific issues during execution. Results on the evaluation set covering diverse presentation-generation scenarios show that DeepPresenter achieves state-of-the-art performance, and the fine-tuned 9B model remains highly competitive at substantially lower cost. Our project is available at: https://github.com/icip-cas/PPTAgent

</details>


### [79] [The AI Research Assistant: Promise, Peril, and a Proof of Concept](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22842)
*Tan Bui-Thanh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文以"Hermite求积公式误差表示与新误差界"的发现为案例，系统记录人机（多款LLM）协作完成数学研究的完整流程。作者主张AI擅长代数符号运算、证明策略系统化探索、文献综述与LaTeX排版等"机械性"环节，但问题设定、数学直觉、证明有效性判断与全部输出的核验必须由人负责。结论是：AI在严谨的怀疑与验证协议下可显著加速数学发现，但无法替代人类学者的领域专长与最终裁决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前关于LLM能否真正参与数学发现的争论两极化：乐观方列举了机器学习发现结理论中的模式、形式化定理证明系统在IMO达到银牌级表现等成果；怀疑方则强调LLM会"幻觉"出貌似正确的错误命题、无法自校验、过度自信，且过度依赖可能削弱人类数学直觉。本文的动机不在于哲学辩论或基准测试，而在于通过真实研究案例的透明记录，回答"AI究竟能否为创造性数学研究做贡献"这一实践性问题。作者强调现有基准（MATH 12,500道竞赛题、709道研究生级数学题评测等）评估的是受控环境下的能力，而真实研究包含迭代、歧路与验证负担，二者结合才能呈现全貌；并指出过往文献中倾向于隐去AI在成果中的角色，故本文以"不寻常的透明度"公开全过程，包括失败案例。

Method: 采用人类—AI分工的结构化协作框架：人负责问题构造与研究方向、数学直觉与战略决策、对AI全部输出的核验、证明有效性与新颖性评估、最终正确性判定；AI（在人类监督下）负责代数与符号运算、系统化证明策略探索、文献检索与综述、LaTeX格式与文档准备、数值示例生成。作者将该分工类比为"资深研究者与能力强但不可靠的研究助理"的关系。方法论载体是一个详细的案例研究：在两个月内针对Hermite求积公式的误差表示与误差界展开人机循环协作，产出并证明了若干新定理，其结果超出纯人工所能达到的范围；同时以脚注形式披露了论文自身写作流程中的AI使用方式（由Claude完成CiSE杂志格式转换与章节重组、摘要与引言重写），并声明所有概念、论证、解释、叙述与批判性评估均由作者本人完成。文中还引用并评述了相关基准研究作为背景对照。

Result: 经验性结论显示，人机协作在Hermite求积误差理论方面取得了超越纯手工工作的实质性成果：新的误差表示形式与误差界被发现、表述并被证明，形成若干定理。能力层面，AI在代数/符号变换、证明路径的系统枚举与穷举、文献检索整合、以及LaTeX排版等机械化任务上表现突出，可在数周内把研究推进到人工难以企及的范围。局限层面同样明确：AI产出的每一个数学步骤都需要人工严格核验，问题形式化与方向设定依赖人类数学直觉，且AI几乎从不对完全错误的结果表达任何不确定性——MATH基准中GPT-2对正误答案均给出近100%置信度、区分正误的AUROC仅68.8%（接近随机50%）；在709道研究生级题目上ChatGPT平均3.20/5、GPT-4为4.15/5，在证明类与复杂计算类问题上退化明显，被判定为"远低于研究生水平"。作者据此提炼出成功协作的模式与研究者必须预判的失效模式。

Conclusion: 论文结论是"折中但有倾向性"的：AI工具并非仅是自动化例行计算，在适当怀疑态度与验证协议下能够实质性加速数学发现；但这一加速是以对人类监督的更高要求为代价的——数学直觉、问题构造、战略判断与全部输出的最终裁决不可外包给AI。作者的实践建议包括：明确分工边界、对每一步AI输出进行人工核验、不掩盖AI在研究中的角色以维持学术诚信、以及在写作层面同样坚持"AI加速机械工作、智识内容归于人类作者"。该文的定位更接近一篇透明化的经验报告/观点案例研究（CiSE专题"AI for Science and Engineering中的争议"），而非提出可复现的新算法或形式化理论；其价值在于为真实科研场景中的人机协作模式与风险提供第一手证据。

Abstract: Can artificial intelligence truly contribute to creative mathematical research, or does it merely automate routine calculations while introducing risks of error? We provide empirical evidence through a detailed case study: the discovery of novel error representations and bounds for Hermite quadrature rules via systematic human-AI collaboration.
  Working with multiple AI assistants, we extended results beyond what manual work achieved, formulating and proving several theorems with AI assistance. The collaboration revealed both remarkable capabilities and critical limitations. AI excelled at algebraic manipulation, systematic proof exploration, literature synthesis, and LaTeX preparation. However, every step required rigorous human verification, mathematical intuition for problem formulation, and strategic direction.
  We document the complete research workflow with unusual transparency, revealing patterns in successful human-AI mathematical collaboration and identifying failure modes researchers must anticipate. Our experience suggests that, when used with appropriate skepticism and verification protocols, AI tools can meaningfully accelerate mathematical discovery while demanding careful human oversight and deep domain expertise.

</details>


### [80] [Towards LLM-Empowered Knowledge Tracing via LLM-Student Hierarchical Behavior Alignment in Hyperbolic Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22879)
*Xingcheng Fu,Shengpeng Wang,Yisen Gao,Xianxian Li,Chunpei Li,Qingyun Sun,Dongran Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 L-HAKT（Large Language Model Hyperbolic Aligned Knowledge Tracing）：以 LLM 双智能体（Teacher 构建分层知识图谱与题目难度、Student 模拟个性化学习行为生成合成数据）为数据与语义来源，用可优化负曲率的双曲几何显式建模知识点树状层级，并在双曲空间中做合成数据与真实行为的对比对齐，最后用双曲-欧氏混合的 HGRU 序列传播器完成知识追踪。在 ASSIST09/ASSIST12/EdNet/Eedi 四个数据集上 AUC/ACC 全面超越 DKT、DKVMN、AKT、SAINT、GKT、GIKT、simpleKT、MIKT 等 14 个基线（如 EdNet AUC 78.23%，较 MIKT 提升约 1.1 点、较 GKT 提升 13.03%），消融实验表明双曲表示与对比对齐两者均有显著贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识追踪（KT）现有方法主要依赖题目ID序列或浅层文本特征，存在三大缺陷：(1) 在欧氏空间中建模，无法表达知识体系天然的树状层级属性，难以刻画认知状态的层级化演化；(2) 未充分利用题目语义中隐含的知识点拓扑关系，相关概念的题目之间联系被割裂；(3) 题目难度感知受个体掌握度干扰，在低水平数据上训练会把中等难度题误判为高难度，掩盖真实的难度评估。此外，LLM 虽可模拟人类学习行为并提供客观难度信息，但仍缺少分层概念表示（基础定义到高阶推理的层级演化）与分层动态认知（不同学生对同一题目存在不同理解偏差），即智能体的通用难度感知与个体学生的动态认知之间存在系统性偏差。

Method: 框架由三部分构成：
(1) LLM 双智能体数据增强。Teacher Agent 通过专用提示模板解析题目图像（Eedi 使用 Qwen-2.5VL）转为文本，抽取并分级知识点（L∈{1,2,3,4}：基础定义→综合推理），据层级建立父子依赖形成树状教学知识图谱，并据知识点层级组合给出客观难度分数。Student Agent 基于学生历史交互序列，用两个模块模拟个性化行为：认知投入模块 Γ_j=σ(W_q·[X_qi; X_cij; t]+b_q)（间隔越短说明投入度越高）与分层遗忘模块 F_j=exp(−λ·L_avg·t_j)（难度层级越高遗忘越快，基础/中等/困难分别对应平缓、中等、最陡），再经 LSTM 更新状态 h_j^s，输出预测 A_j 与推理路径 P_j=[c^(1),…,c^(k)→A_j]。
(2) 双曲编码与对齐。构建含题目—知识点边与知识点层级依赖边 E_hie={(c_i,c_j)|L_ci<L_cj} 的异质图；通过指数映射将欧氏嵌入投影至超双曲面模型，对真实图与合成图分别使用独立曲率 κ_real、κ_syn；采用关系感知的多头双曲图注意力聚合（切空间 log/exp 运算）。
(3) 双曲知识追踪。序列传播阶段用对数映射将题目—知识点双曲嵌入与隐状态投影到原点切空间，与响应 R_t 及难度/层级差分表示 D^t=φ(W_d·q_diff + W_L·c̄^L) 融合，送入 HGRU 递推，再经指数映射回到双曲空间得到 H_t^ℋ；训练损失 L_total = L_KT(BCE) + α·L_con，α 在 [0.0001, 0.001, 0.01, 0.1, 1] 中搜索。对比损失 L_con 以真实/合成共享的题目—概念对为正样本，其余为负样本，用余弦相似度与温度系数 τ 构造 InfoNCE 形式损失。
超参数：向量维度 d 设为知识点数，表示学习模块 2 层，单张 A100 40GB，数据 8:2 划分，指标为 AUC 与 ACC。

Result: 表1：L-HAKT 在四个数据集全面最优。ASSIST09：AUC 80.22±0.30 / ACC 75.10±0.04（次优 MIKT 79.38/74.54）；ASSIST12：80.27±0.40 / 77.10±0.14（MIKT 78.65/76.52，AKT 78.21/75.23）；EdNet：78.23±0.05 / 75.21±0.01（MIKT 77.10/74.22）；Eedi：80.29±0.02 / 73.49±0.03（MIKT 79.59/72.67，GIKT 79.68/73.02）。相比图模型 GKT 提升 3.67%–13.03%（ACC）。表2 数据规模：ASSIST09 4160 学生/15643 题/167 概念/206631 交互；ASSIST12 5000/36054/242/713123；EdNet 5000/11700/1830/1147423；Eedi 5000/26702/1050/586234。
消融（以 GKT 为骨干，记作 L-HVKT）：w/o hyp（去双曲表示）提升 1.06%–10.59%，w/o con（去对比对齐）提升 0.40%–9.10%，完整模型提升 2.53%–13.03%，说明双曲几何与对比对齐均必要，且单纯加入合成数据仅带来数据增强收益，真正校准分布需要显式对齐。图4 验证：仅真实题目—知识点边 < 加入 Student Agent 合成数据 < 再加 Teacher 知识图谱与双曲对比对齐，性能逐级提升；教师生成的知识图谱片段呈现符合教学逻辑的层级依赖（如 limit(2)→derivative(2)→differential equations(4)）。此外，论文给出 H-all、H-ek、H-e、H-k 四种关系组合的超曲率度量（数值越低越贴合树状/双曲结构），作为层级性的经验性佐证。

Conclusion: L-HAKT 首次提出 LLM 师生双智能体协作框架，弥补传统数据中"学生思维路径"的建模盲区；创新性地引入双曲几何并通过曲率优化显式建模知识点层级，精确刻画不同层级知识点的学习曲线形态差异（基础概念渐进掌握 vs 高阶知识点的突变式跃迁）；基于对齐后的层级数据设计双曲知识状态追踪器，利用层级传播特性敏感捕捉高阶知识点的掌握跃迁。实验在四个真实教育数据集上验证了框架有效性，双曲嵌入可视化显示知识层级与学习曲线形态存在相关性。局限与展望：依赖 LLM 解析题目图像与知识点的质量与成本；曲率与 α 等超参需搜索；未来工作将探索面向教学优化的双曲学习生成，以及结合模型空间实现细粒度诊断。

Abstract: Knowledge Tracing (KT) diagnoses students' concept mastery through continuous learning state monitoring in education.Existing methods primarily focus on studying behavioral sequences based on ID or textual information.While existing methods rely on ID-based sequences or shallow textual features, they often fail to capture (1) the hierarchical evolution of cognitive states and (2) individualized problem difficulty perception due to limited semantic modeling. Therefore, this paper proposes a Large Language Model Hyperbolic Aligned Knowledge Tracing(L-HAKT). First, the teacher agent deeply parses question semantics and explicitly constructs hierarchical dependencies of knowledge points; the student agent simulates learning behaviors to generate synthetic data. Then, contrastive learning is performed between synthetic and real data in hyperbolic space to reduce distribution differences in key features such as question difficulty and forgetting patterns. Finally, by optimizing hyperbolic curvature, we explicitly model the tree-like hierarchical structure of knowledge points, precisely characterizing differences in learning curve morphology for knowledge points at different levels. Extensive experiments on four real-world educational datasets validate the effectiveness of our Large Language Model Hyperbolic Aligned Knowledge Tracing (L-HAKT) framework.

</details>


### [81] [OmniGAIA: Towards Native Omni-Modal AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22897)
*Xiaoxi Li,Wenxiang Jiao,Jiarui Jin,Shijian Wang,Guanting Dong,Jiajie Jin,Hao Wang,Yinuo Wang,Ji-Rong Wen,Yuan Lu,Zhicheng Dou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发布 OmniGAIA——首个面向"原生全模态智能体"的高难度评测基准（360 道任务、9 个真实领域、含视频+音频与图像+音频两类输入、要求多跳推理与多轮工具调用、开放式可验证答案），全部由"全模态事件图驱动"的合成流水线构建；同时提出 OmniAtlas 这一原生全模态基座智能体，采用工具集成推理（TIR）范式与"主动感知"机制，通过后见之明引导的树搜索轨迹合成、轨迹级掩码 SFT 与新提出的 OmniDPO 细粒度纠错进行训练。实验显示：最强闭源模型 Gemini-3-Pro 仅得 62.5 Pass@1，最强开源基线 Qwen3-Omni 仅 13.3，而 OmniAtlas 配方将 Qwen3-Omni 提升至 20.8。核心结论是：决定性瓶颈在工具使用策略与长程推理，而非参数规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态大模型研究仍以"双模态"（视觉-语言、音频-语言）为主，虽然 Qwen3-Omni 等全模态基座模型开始统一文本/视觉/音频，但研究重心仍停留在感知层面，"工具集成 + 智能体式长程推理"几乎空白。同时评测滞后：OmniBench、WorldSense、Daily-Omni、UNO-Bench 等现有基准大多为短时长、以感知/多选为主，无法衡量多跳全模态推理、多轮外部工具调用与可验证的开放式答案（GAIA、AV-Odyssey 亦不支持视频+音频与工具的组合）。因此需要一个能同时压测"原生全模态感知 + 深度推理 + 多轮工具执行"的基准，并配套一个可复现的提升开源模型智能体能力的训练配方。此外，人工智能助手需要在长视频/高分辨率图像上进行"按需看/按需听"，而全量下采样会丢失关键细节，这构成方法层面的直接动机。

Method: 一、OmniGAIA 基准构建（事件图驱动流水线）：①数据采集——视频取自 FineVideo(43K)、LongVideoBench(~1K)、LongVideo-Reason(~1K)，图像取自 COCO 2017(122K)，音频轨来自 FineVideo；②用 Gemini-3-Flash 挖掘细粒度时序信号（视频切片级+全片描述、ASR/说话人分离/音频事件检测与场景标记、图像 OCR/物体与人脸/整体描述）；③用 DeepSeek-V3.2 构建"全模态事件图"，表达实体/事件及跨模态关系，覆盖分支、级联与混合拓扑；④"智能体式事件图扩展"——以 DeepSeek-V3.2 为探索智能体，配备跨模态检索（search_related_{video,audio,image}_info）、网页知识（web_search、page_browser）、外部视觉（web_image_search、visual_question_answering）与计算（code_executor）工具，主动寻找"下一跳证据"并回连图；⑤事件模糊化生成 QA——把长推理路径上的关键节点/边替换为"模糊实体"或遮蔽关键属性，迫使模型走完整逻辑链；⑥质量控制——DeepSeek-V3.2 + Gemini-3-Pro 组成的 LLM 评审委员会筛查（自然度、模态/工具不可替代性、答案正确性与唯一性），可选难度扩展，再由 3 名研究生级评审逐条人工核验。产出 360 题、9 领域、视频 20–2352s、音频 20–657s、开放式答案。

二、OmniAtlas 智能体：遵循工具集成推理范式，交替生成思考与工具调用，形式化轨迹 τ=[(s_t,a_t,o_t)]，检测到工具调用 token 即暂停生成、执行工具并将观测 o_t 回填上下文；支持"主动全模态感知"工具 read_video(t_start,t_end)、read_audio(t_start,t_end)、read_image(crop_box)，实现"look/listen-where-needed"而非全量降采样。

三、训练：先由 Gemini-3-Flash 将原始多模态输入转为细粒度文本描述，再用"后见之明引导的树探索"合成工具集成轨迹（DeepSeek-V3.2 采样 k=3 个候选续写，Gemini-3-Flash 结合真值剪枝，仅保留成功轨迹，共 2,156 条）；采用掩码 SFT（仅对智能体自生成的思考与工具调用 token 计损失，屏蔽工具观测）；再提出 OmniDPO——让 Gemini-3-Flash 在失败轨迹中定位"首个错误步骤"并生成修正前缀，构成 (τ_win, τ_lose) 偏好对，用掩码 DPO 目标做细粒度纠错。基于 LlamaFactory 实现，Qwen2.5-Omni-3B/7B 与 Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B 全参数微调（含视觉塔与投影层），4 节点×8×H20-141GB。

四、评测：LLM-as-a-Judge（DeepSeek-V3.2）+ 精确匹配两阶段协议，报告 Pass@1，并含细粒度错误分类、工具调用分布、原生感知 vs 工具化感知的对照消融。

Result: 主结果（Pass@1）：闭源 Gemini-3-Pro 62.5（Easy 78.7 / Med. 61.9 / Hard 38.5）、Gemini-3-Flash 51.7、Gemini-2.5-Pro 30.8、Gemini-2.5-Flash-Lite 8.6；开源 Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B 13.3、Ming-Flash-Omni 8.3、LongCat-Flash-Omni-560B 11.1、Qwen2.5-Omni-7B 3.6 等。闭源-开源差距约 4.7×；单纯扩参数无效（560B 的 LongCat 低于 30B 的 Qwen3-Omni）。OmniAtlas 带来一致增益：Qwen3-Omni 13.3→20.8（+7.5），OmniAtlas-Qwen2.5-7B 3.6→13.3（约 3.7×）。消融显示 SFT 贡献主要增量（13.3→18.9，工具误用率 81.1%→65.3%），OmniDPO 再补 18.9→20.8（工具误用 59.4%、推理错误 64.4%）。错误分析：无效工具使用占 35.3%–91.9%、推理错误 15.8%–79.7% 为主要失败模式，Hard 子集上开源模型工具误用近 90%–96%；视觉/音频感知错误仍达约 30%–50%，是剩余瓶颈。工具调用分析：几乎不调工具的模型近乎全败，调用数 >10–20 的长尾仍大量失败（"空转"），OmniAtlas 明显从"少调用"转向更主动调用。感知消融：对强模型 Gemini-3-Flash，原生全模态感知最优（51.7，4.4 次调用），改用感知工具后降至 43.3–50.0 且调用升至 6.8–9.4；对弱模型 Qwen-3-Omni，感知工具能改善 Easy/Med（19.7→24.6、10.6→15.0）但 Hard 反而下降（9.0→3.9/5.1/7.7）。案例研究归纳出两类主导失败模式：欠调用（不取证）与工具查询漂移（被"芝加哥桥"先验带偏、在错误事实上做正确计算），成功轨迹模式为"位置优先锚定 → 假设检验与验证 → 再计算 → 答案规范化"。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献有四点：(1) 提出 OmniGAIA——覆盖视频/图像/音频、多领域、多跳推理、多轮工具使用与开放式答案的原生全模态智能体基准，揭示现有模型在长程感知-推理-工具链上的巨大缺口；(2) 提出可扩展的"事件图驱动构建流水线"，可从真实世界数据系统化合成既难又可解的任务；(3) 提出 OmniAtlas 及一套可落地的训练配方（后见之明引导树探索轨迹合成 + 轨迹级掩码 SFT + OmniDPO 细粒度纠错），显著提升开源骨干的工具使用与推理能力（Qwen3-Omni 13.3→20.8）；(4) 提供细粒度错误归因、工具调用行为与感知范式（原生 vs 工具化）的系统分析，指出关键瓶颈与未来方向。总体结论：决定全模态智能体性能的关键是有效的工具使用策略与长程推理，而非单纯参数规模；原生感知应是强模型默认路径，工具化感知仅作为弱模型或缺失模态的回退方案；Hard 级多跳任务与感知能力仍是主要待解问题。未来工作方向包括：全模态智能体强化学习（Omni-modal Agentic RL）、可扩展的 Omni-modal MCP 工具服务、以及面向物理世界的全模态具身智能体基准与模型。

Abstract: Human intelligence naturally intertwines omni-modal perception -- spanning vision, audio, and language -- with complex reasoning and tool usage to interact with the world. However, current multi-modal LLMs are primarily confined to bi-modal interactions (e.g., vision-language), lacking the unified cognitive capabilities required for general AI assistants. To bridge this gap, we introduce OmniGAIA, a comprehensive benchmark designed to evaluate omni-modal agents on tasks necessitating deep reasoning and multi-turn tool execution across video, audio, and image modalities. Constructed via a novel omni-modal event graph approach, OmniGAIA synthesizes complex, multi-hop queries derived from real-world data that require cross-modal reasoning and external tool integration. Furthermore, we propose OmniAtlas, a native omni-modal foundation agent under tool-integrated reasoning paradigm with active omni-modal perception. Trained on trajectories synthesized via a hindsight-guided tree exploration strategy and OmniDPO for fine-grained error correction, OmniAtlas effectively enhances the tool-use capabilities of existing open-source models. This work marks a step towards next-generation native omni-modal AI assistants for real-world scenarios.

</details>


### [82] [General Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22953)
*Elron Bandel,Asaf Yehudai,Lilach Eden,Yehoshua Sagron,Yotam Perlitz,Elad Venezian,Natalia Razinkov,Natan Ergas,Shlomit Shachor Ifergan,Segev Shlomov,Michal Jacovi,Leshem Choshen,Liat Ein-Dor,Yoav Katz,Michal Shmueli-Scheuer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是首个系统性「通用智能体（general agent）」评测研究。作者提出统一协议（Unified Protocol）作为智能体与基准之间的「窄腰」中介层，实现 Exgentic 评测框架，使任意未经改动的智能体可直接接入任意异构基准；并在 5 种智能体架构 × 5 个骨干模型 × 6 个基准（软件工程、客服、深研、个人助理）的全因子设计上发布首个「Open General Agent Leaderboard」（约 $20K，550 实例/配置，共 150 个配置）。结论：通用智能体无需任何按域调优即可在每个域取得非平凡成绩，并在 6 个基准中的 4 个上与重度定制的领域专用智能体统计不可区分；骨干模型选择解释 27.8% 的成绩方差而架构仅 0.5%，但单一模型内架构选择仍可造成最高 12pp（开源权重模型 14–18pp）的摆动；开源权重模型出现前沿闭源模型没有的「通用性下沉」（架构下沉与基准下沉）；失败模式分析能区分聚合分数无法区分的架构差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用智能体（能在陌生环境中无需领域工程即可完成任务的系统）被广泛期待，但现实中的智能体几乎都是专用化的，OpenAI Agents SDK、Claude Code 等新实现虽暗示了更通用能力，却没有任何系统性评测。两大障碍使得「同一未修改智能体跨多基准」的研究无法进行：(i) 框架缺口——现有 harness 要求逐基准接线工具/沙箱/求解器（Inspect 的 Task(solver=...) 模式），或强制单一传输协议（BrowserGym 仅 web、Harbor 仅 CLI），实际评测的是被削弱的智能体；(ii) 基准缺口——现有基准把任务信息、可供性（affordances）与交互假设隐式编码进去，假定人工完成集成，且缺少可供通用智能体接口使用的显式信息。因此此前没有研究能在相同基准、相同模型下比较不同架构，导致「智能体本身」的贡献无法被隔离，而社区已多次呼吁把通用智能体评测作为独立研究目标。

Method: 统一协议（Unified Protocol）：每个实例由 task（要做什么）、context（需要知道什么）、actions（可做什么，含类型化参数，可返回任意类型观测）三字段构成，并允许可选指定一个 message 动作与一个 final-answer/finish 动作；该三字段表示是最小化的「窄腰」，由现有 agent/benchmark 通信模式调研归纳而来，可无损表达各协议的语义。Exgentic 框架：以黑盒方式运行原生 agent 与原生 benchmark，通过外部 adapter（而非侵入式改代码）做双向协议翻译——agent 侧将 actions 映射为 Python 函数、OpenAI tool 或 MCP tool；benchmark 侧把任务定义与接口翻译为协议动作，并为每个基准从其官方参考 agent 实现（如 mini-SWE agent、τ² LLMAgent、AppWorld minimal_agent、OpenAI search-agent）反推出协议语义，剔除依赖特定协议形状的指令。评测规模：5 架构（ReAct、ReAct Short=带工具短选的 ReAct、Smolagent 代码生成、OpenAI Solo（Agents SDK v0.7.0 solo+MCP）、Claude Code v2.1.7（Docker 内不改内部逻辑））× 5 骨干模型（Claude Opus 4.5、Gemini 3 Pro、GPT 5.2、DeepSeek-V3.2、Kimi-K2.5）× 6 基准（AppWorld、BrowseComp+、SWE-Bench Verified、τ²-Bench Airline/Retail/Telecom），每基准 100 题（Airline 50），共 150 配置、每配置 550 实例、单任务 100 步上限，总花费约 $20K；推理一律使用各 provider 文档默认采样参数以避免超参混杂。指标：成功率、每任务美元成本、平均步数、基准加权均值（三个 τ² 子域各占 1/12）、成本效率（score/$）。统计：η² 方差分解、paired t 检验、pooled McNemar、Wilson 95% CI、Spearman 秩相关、两比例 z 检验，并用 Benjamini–Hochberg / Benjamini–Yekutieli 在 α=0.05 控制多重比较；另做 schema guard / 工具短选消融、失败运行的成本-步数对比，并把 ErrorMap 适配到轨迹上对 2,868 个失败会话做 27 类失败模式归因（gpt-5.5 判定，100 条抽样验证）。全部代码、协议、适配器、排行榜与轨迹开源，并提供逐条数值的审计脚本。

Result: 排行榜由 OpenAI Solo + Claude Opus 4.5 领跑（加权均值 0.73，$8.5/题），前三名均为 Claude Opus 4.5 组合且差距在 6pp 内，前十全部为闭源组合。五点核心发现：(1) 通用智能体无需按域调优即可在全部域取得非平凡成绩；(2) 模型选择解释 27.8% 方差而架构仅 0.5%（约 58× 差距，闭源子集；加回开源权重模型后架构主效应变得显著 F(4,136)=3.82, p=0.006），但同一模型内架构可摆动 12pp（开源权重 14–18pp），且 (model, architecture) 交互项达 5.4%，说明最优架构依赖模型；(3) 在 SWE-Bench Verified、τ²-Airline、τ²-Retail、AppWorld 上，最佳通用配置与顶级领域专用智能体分数在统计噪声内不可区分（0.81 vs 0.79、0.74 vs 0.73、0.85 vs 0.86、0.70 vs 0.73），仅 BrowseComp+ 与 τ²-Telecom 仍由专用体领先（0.61 vs 0.80、0.88 vs 0.98），且没有单一架构全局最优；(4) 通用性下沉：τ²-Bench 上出现架构下沉（Kimi-K2.5 同一模型在不同架构间 0.83→0.00，94% 会话首轮违反「仅用工具调用」协议而零步终止，闭源仅 1.7%，z=+41.4, p<1e-15），AppWorld 上出现基准下沉（所有开源权重架构崩溃，最高 0.13 vs 0.70），GPT 5.2 因 128 工具上限在 468 个动作上多个配置为 0.00、工具短选仅救回 0.22；SWE-Bench 与 BrowseComp+ 无下沉；(5) 失败模式可区分架构：χ²(196)=328.5, p<1e-10, Cramér's V=0.17，Claude Code/OpenAI Solo 过度代表「过早终止」，ReAct/ReAct Short 过度代表「证据检索缺失」，Smolagent 以「搜索恢复与适应」为主；失败运行普遍比成功运行更耗资源（ReAct 高出 +54%，AppWorld 上 +111%）。此外工具短选在 5 个模型中 4 个带来提升（ReAct+GPT 5.2 提升 5.5pp；ReAct+Claude Opus 4.5 提升较小但每任务省 $1.97，DeepSeek 例外），schema guard 与榜单前三架构相关（属观察性模式）；成本效率跨配置差异约 30×（最优 2.43 score/$，最差 0.08 score/$），最高分配置的性价比仅 0.09。

Conclusion: 论文主张：通用智能体是一条可行且有竞争力的路线——无需任何按域工程即可在多数基准上达到与重度定制领域专家相当的水平，因此应以「同一未修改智能体跨多环境」为一等研究目标，并配套协议与公开基础设施（Unified Protocol、Exgentic、Open General Agent Leaderboard）作为社区基座。后续方向：训练模型使其在不同架构间稳定工作（消除开源权重模型的架构下沉）、继续挖掘简单组件（schema guard、工具短选、抗过早终止）带来的跨模型收益、以及把成本效率作为第三条开放轴（当前无普适最优，效率跨度 30×）。作者也坦承局限：仅 5 个模型（含 2 个开源权重检查点）、5 个架构、6 个基准、约 $20K 成本；单单元格只跑一次、采用 provider 默认采样，未测重跑方差；仅支持类型化离散动作，多模态与连续动作（像素级 computer use、机器人控制）需扩展协议；沿用各基准原生自动评测器，未做人工评估（BrowseComp+、SWE-Bench 尤甚）；提示词与 first-turn 指令仍需手工定制，一定程度上违背「零人工」初衷。论文邀请社区贡献新智能体、新基准与协议适配器，共同把该排行榜扩展为衡量通用化进展的公共基准。

Abstract: The promise of general-purpose agents - systems that perform tasks in unfamiliar environments without domain-specific engineering - remains largely unrealized. Existing agents are predominantly specialized, and while emerging implementations like OpenAI SDK Agent and Claude Code hint at broader capabilities, no systematic evaluation of their general performance has been pursued. Current agentic benchmarks assume domain-specific integration, encoding task information in ways that preclude fair evaluation of general agents. This paper frames general-agent evaluation as a first-class research objective. We propose conceptual principles for such evaluation, a Unified Protocol enabling agent-benchmark integration, and Exgentic - a practical framework for general agent evaluation. We benchmark five prominent agent implementations across six environments as the first Open General Agent Leaderboard. Our experiments show that general agents generalize across diverse environments, achieving performance comparable to domain-specific agents without any environment-specific tuning. We release our evaluation protocol, framework, and leaderboard to establish a foundation for systematic research on general-purpose agents.

</details>


### [83] [FactGuard: Agentic Video Misinformation Detection via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22963)
*Zehao Li,Hongwei Yu,Hao Jiang,Qiang Sheng,Yilong Xu,Baolong Bi,Yang Li,Zhenlong Yuan,Yujun Cai,Zhaoqi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have substantially advanced video misinformation detection through unified multimodal reasoning, but they often rely on fixed-depth inference and place excessive trust in internally generated assumptions, particularly in scenarios where critical evidence is sparse, fragmented, or requires external verification. To address these limitations, we propose FactGuard, an agentic framework for video misinformation detection that formulates verification as an iterative reasoning process built upon MLLMs. FactGuard explicitly assesses task ambiguity and selectively invokes external tools to acquire critical evidence, enabling progressive refinement of reasoning trajectories. To further strengthen this capability, we introduce a two-stage training strategy that combines domain-specific agentic supervised fine-tuning with decision-aware reinforcement learning to optimize tool usage and calibrate risk-sensitive decision making. Extensive experiments on FakeSV, FakeTT, and FakeVV demonstrate FactGuard's state-of-the-art performance and validate its excellent robustness and generalization capacity.

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have substantially advanced video misinformation detection through unified multimodal reasoning, but they often rely on fixed-depth inference and place excessive trust in internally generated assumptions, particularly in scenarios where critical evidence is sparse, fragmented, or requires external verification. To address these limitations, we propose FactGuard, an agentic framework for video misinformation detection that formulates verification as an iterative reasoning process built upon MLLMs. FactGuard explicitly assesses task ambiguity and selectively invokes external tools to acquire critical evidence, enabling progressive refinement of reasoning trajectories. To further strengthen this capability, we introduce a two-stage training strategy that combines domain-specific agentic supervised fine-tuning with decision-aware reinforcement learning to optimize tool usage and calibrate risk-sensitive decision making. ### [84] [Certified Circuits: Stability Guarantees for Mechanistic Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22968)
*Alaa Anani,Tobias Lorenz,Bernt Schiele,Mario Fritz,Jonas Fischer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Understanding how neural networks arrive at their predictions is essential for debugging, auditing, and deployment. Mechanistic interpretability pursues this goal by identifying circuits - minimal subnetworks responsible for specific behaviors. However, existing circuit discovery methods are brittle: circuits depend strongly on the chosen concept dataset and often fail to transfer out-of-distribution, raising doubts whether they capture concept or dataset-specific artifacts. We introduce Certified Circuits, which provide provable stability guarantees for circuit discovery. Our framework wraps any black-box discovery algorithm with randomized data subsampling to certify that circuit component inclusion decisions are invariant to bounded edit-distance perturbations of the concept dataset. Unstable neurons are abstained from, yielding circuits that are more compact and more accurate. On ImageNet and OOD datasets, certified circuits achieve up to 91% higher accuracy while using 45% fewer neurons, and remain reliable where baselines degrade. Certified Circuits puts circuit discovery on formal ground by producing mechanistic explanations that are provably stable and better aligned with the target concept. Code will be released soon!

### [85] [SPM-Bench: Benchmarking Large Language Models for Scanning Probe Microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22971)
*Peiyao Xiao,Xiaogang Li,Chengliang Xu,Jiayi Wang,Ben Wang,Zichao Chen,Zeyu Wang,Kejun Yu,Yueqian Chen,Xulin Liu,Wende Xiao,Bing Zhao,Hu Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: As LLMs achieved breakthroughs in general reasoning, their proficiency in specialized scientific domains reveals pronounced gaps in existing benchmarks due to data contamination, insufficient complexity, and prohibitive human labor costs. Here we present SPM-Bench, an original, PhD-level multimodal benchmark specifically designed for scanning probe microscopy (SPM). We propose a fully automated data synthesis pipeline that ensures both high authority and low-cost. By employing Anchor-Gated Sieve (AGS) technology, we efficiently extract high-value image-text pairs from arXiv and journal papers published between 2023 and 2025. Through a hybrid cloud-local architecture where VLMs return only spatial coordinates "llbox" for local high-fidelity cropping, our pipeline achieves extreme token savings while maintaining high dataset purity. To accurately and objectively evaluate the performance of the LLMs, we introduce the Strict Imperfection Penalty F1 (SIP-F1) score. This metric not only establishes a rigorous capability hierarchy but also, for the first time, quantifies model "personalities" (Conservative, Aggressive, Gambler, or Wise). By correlating these results with model-reported confidence and perceived difficulty, we expose the true reasoning boundaries of current AI in complex physical scenarios. These insights establish SPM-Bench as a generalizable paradigm for automated scientific data synthesis.

### [86] [Modeling Expert AI Diagnostic Alignment via Immutable Inference Snapshots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22973)
*Dimitrios P. Panagoulias,Evangelia-Aikaterini Tsichrintzi,Georgios Savvidis,Evridiki Tsoureli-Nikita*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Human-in-the-loop validation is essential in safety-critical clinical AI, yet the transition between initial model inference and expert correction is rarely analyzed as a structured signal. We introduce a diagnostic alignment framework in which the AI-generated image based report is preserved as an immutable inference state and systematically compared with the physician-validated outcome. The inference pipeline integrates a vision-enabled large language model, BERT- based medical entity extraction, and a Sequential Language Model Inference (SLMI) step to enforce domain-consistent refinement prior to expert review. Evaluation on 21 dermatological cases (21 complete AI physician pairs) em- ployed a four-level concordance framework comprising exact primary match rate (PMR), semantic similarity-adjusted rate (AMR), cross-category alignment, and Comprehensive Concordance Rate (CCR). Exact agreement reached 71.4% and remained unchanged under semantic similarity (t = 0.60), while structured cross-category and differential overlap analysis yielded 100% comprehensive concordance (95% CI: [83.9%, 100%]). No cases demonstrated complete diagnostic divergence. These findings show that binary lexical evaluation substantially un- derestimates clinically meaningful alignment. Modeling expert validation as a structured transformation enables signal-aware quantification of correction dynamics and supports traceable, human aligned evaluation of image based clinical decision support systems.

Abstract: Human-in-the-loop validation is essential in safety-critical clinical AI, yet the transition between initial model inference and expert correction is rarely analyzed as a structured signal. We introduce a diagnostic alignment framework in which the AI-generated image based report is preserved as an immutable inference state and systematically compared with the physician-validated outcome. The inference pipeline integrates a vision-enabled large language model, BERT- based medical entity extraction, and a Sequential Language Model Inference (SLMI) step to enforce domain-consistent refinement prior to expert review. Evaluation on 21 dermatological cases (21 complete AI physician pairs) em- ployed a four-level concordance framework comprising exact primary match rate (PMR), semantic similarity-adjusted rate (AMR), cross-category alignment, and Comprehensive Concordance Rate (CCR). Exact agreement reached 71.4% and remained unchanged under semantic similarity (t = 0.60), while structured cross-category and differential overlap analysis yielded 100% comprehensive concordance (95% CI: [83.9%, 100%]). No cases demonstrated complete diagnostic divergence. These findings show that binary lexical evaluation substantially un- derestimates clinically meaningful alignment. Modeling expert validation as a structured transformation enables signal-aware quantification of correction dynamics and supports traceable, human aligned evaluation of image based clinical decision support systems.

</details>


### [87] [RepSPD: Enhancing SPD Manifold Representation in EEGs via Dynamic Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22981)
*Haohui Jia,Zheng Chen,Lingwei Zhu,Xu Cao,Yasuko Matsubara,Takashi Matsubara,Yasushi Sakurai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Decoding brain activity from electroencephalography (EEG) is crucial for neuroscience and clinical applications. Among recent advances in deep learning for EEG, geometric learning stands out as its theoretical underpinnings on symmetric positive definite (SPD) allows revealing structural connectivity analysis in a physics-grounded manner. However, current SPD-based methods focus predominantly on statistical aggregation of EEGs, with frequency-specific synchronization and local topological structures of brain regions neglected. Given this, we propose RepSPD, a novel geometric deep learning (GDL)-based model. RepSPD implements a cross-attention mechanism on the Riemannian manifold to modulate the geometric attributes of SPD with graph-derived functional connectivity features. On top of this, we introduce a global bidirectional alignment strategy to reshape tangent-space embeddings, mitigating geometric distortions caused by curvature and thereby enhancing geometric consistency. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our proposed framework significantly outperforms existing EEG representation methods, exhibiting superior robustness and generalization capabilities.

### [88] [Obscure but Effective: Classical Chinese Jailbreak Prompt Optimization via Bio-Inspired Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22983)
*Xun Huang,Simeng Qin,Xiaoshuang Jia,Ranjie Duan,Huanqian Yan,Zhitao Zeng,Fei Yang,Yang Liu,Xiaojun Jia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: As Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used, their security risks have drawn increasing attention. Existing research reveals that LLMs are highly susceptible to jailbreak attacks, with effectiveness varying across language contexts. This paper investigates the role of classical Chinese in jailbreak attacks. Owing to its conciseness and obscurity, classical Chinese can partially bypass existing safety constraints, exposing notable vulnerabilities in LLMs. Based on this observation, this paper proposes a framework, CC-BOS, for the automatic generation of classical Chinese adversarial prompts based on multi-dimensional fruit fly optimization, facilitating efficient and automated jailbreak attacks in black-box settings. Prompts are encoded into eight policy dimensions-covering role, behavior, mechanism, metaphor, expression, knowledge, trigger pattern and context; and iteratively refined via smell search, visual search, and cauchy mutation. This design enables efficient exploration of the search space, thereby enhancing the effectiveness of black-box jailbreak attacks. To enhance readability and evaluation accuracy, we further design a classical Chinese to English translation module. Extensive experiments demonstrate that effectiveness of the proposed CC-BOS, consistently outperforming state-of-the-art jailbreak attack methods.

### [89] [Learning-based Multi-agent Race Strategies in Formula 1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.23056)
*Giona Fieni,Joschua Wüthrich,Marc-Philippe Neumann,Christopher H. Onder*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: In Formula 1, race strategies are adapted according to evolving race conditions and competitors' actions. This paper proposes a reinforcement learning approach for multi-agent race strategy optimization. Agents learn to balance energy management, tire degradation, aerodynamic interaction, and pit-stop decisions. Building on a pre-trained single-agent policy, we introduce an interaction module that accounts for the behavior of competitors. The combination of the interaction module and a self-play training scheme generates competitive policies, and agents are ranked based on their relative performance. Results show that the agents adapt pit timing, tire selection, and energy allocation in response to opponents, achieving robust and consistent race performance. Because the framework relies only on information available during real races, it can support race strategists' decisions before and during races.

### [90] [Enhancing CVRP Solver through LLM-driven Automatic Heuristic Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.23092)
*Zhuoliang Xie,Fei Liu,Zhenkun Wang,Qingfu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: The Capacitated Vehicle Routing Problem (CVRP), a fundamental combinatorial optimization challenge, focuses on optimizing fleet operations under vehicle capacity constraints. While extensively studied in operational research, the NP-hard nature of CVRP continues to pose significant computational challenges, particularly for large-scale instances. This study presents AILS-AHD (Adaptive Iterated Local Search with Automatic Heuristic Design), a novel approach that leverages Large Language Models (LLMs) to revolutionize CVRP solving. Our methodology integrates an evolutionary search framework with LLMs to dynamically generate and optimize ruin heuristics within the AILS method. Additionally, we introduce an LLM-based acceleration mechanism to enhance computational efficiency. Comprehensive experimental evaluations against state-of-the-art solvers, including AILS-II and HGS, demonstrate the superior performance of AILS-AHD across both moderate and large-scale instances. Notably, our approach establishes new best-known solutions for 8 out of 10 instances in the CVRPLib large-scale benchmark, underscoring the potential of LLM-driven heuristic design in advancing the field of vehicle routing optimization.

### [91] [Three AI-agents walk into a bar . . . . `Lord of the Flies' tribalism emerges among smart AI-Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.23093)
*Dhwanil M. Mori,Neil F. Johnson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Near-future infrastructure systems may be controlled by autonomous AI agents that repeatedly request access to limited resources such as energy, bandwidth, or computing power. We study a simplified version of this setting using a framework where N AI-agents independently decide at each round whether to request one unit from a system with fixed capacity C. An AI version of "Lord of the Flies" arises in which controlling tribes emerge with their own collective character and identity. The LLM agents do not reduce overload or improve resource use, and often perform worse than if they were flipping coins to make decisions. Three main tribal types emerge: Aggressive (27.3%), Conservative (24.7%), and Opportunistic (48.1%). The more capable AI-agents actually increase the rate of systemic failure. Overall, our findings show that smarter AI-agents can behave dumber as a result of forming tribes.

### [92] [Multi-Agent Large Language Model Based Emotional Detoxification Through Personalized Intensity Control for Consumer Protection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.23123)
*Keito Inoshita*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: In the attention economy, sensational content exposes consumers to excessive emotional stimulation, hindering calm decision-making. This study proposes Multi-Agent LLM-based Emotional deToxification (MALLET), a multi-agent information sanitization system consisting of four agents: Emotion Analysis, Emotion Adjustment, Balance Monitoring, and Personal Guide. The Emotion Analysis Agent quantifies stimulus intensity using a 6-emotion BERT classifier, and the Emotion Adjustment Agent rewrites texts into two presentation modes, BALANCED (neutralized text) and COOL (neutralized text + supplementary text), using an LLM. The Balance Monitoring Agent aggregates weekly information consumption patterns and generates personalized advice, while the Personal Guide Agent recommends a presentation mode according to consumer sensitivity. Experiments on 800 AG News articles demonstrated significant stimulus score reduction (up to 19.3%) and improved emotion balance while maintaining semantic preservation. Near-zero correlation between stimulus reduction and semantic preservation confirmed that the two are independently controllable. Category-level analysis revealed substantial reduction (17.8-33.8%) in Sports, Business, and Sci/Tech, whereas the effect was limited in the World category, where facts themselves are inherently high-stimulus. The proposed system provides a framework for supporting calm information reception of consumers without restricting access to the original text.

### [93] [On Sample-Efficient Generalized Planning via Learned Transition Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.23148)
*Nitin Gupta,Vishal Pallagani,John A. Aydin,Biplav Srivastava*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Generalized planning studies the construction of solution strategies that generalize across families of planning problems sharing a common domain model, formally defined by a transition function $γ: S \times A \rightarrow S$. Classical approaches achieve such generalization through symbolic abstractions and explicit reasoning over $γ$. In contrast, recent Transformer-based planners, such as PlanGPT and Plansformer, largely cast generalized planning as direct action-sequence prediction, bypassing explicit transition modeling. While effective on in-distribution instances, these approaches typically require large datasets and model sizes, and often suffer from state drift in long-horizon settings due to the absence of explicit world-state evolution. In this work, we formulate generalized planning as a transition-model learning problem, in which a neural model explicitly approximates the successor-state function $\hatγ \approx γ$ and generates plans by rolling out symbolic state trajectories. Instead of predicting actions directly, the model autoregressively predicts intermediate world states, thereby learning the domain dynamics as an implicit world model. To study size-invariant generalization and sample efficiency, we systematically evaluate multiple state representations and neural architectures, including relational graph encodings. Our results show that learning explicit transition models yields higher out-of-distribution satisficing-plan success than direct action-sequence prediction in multiple domains, while achieving these gains with significantly fewer training instances and smaller models. This is an extended version of a short paper accepted at ICAPS 2026 under the same title.
